1b1(phal chen): @#//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba//bam po dang po//dum bu bzhi pa/
1b2(phal chen): de nas byang chub sems dpa' tshogs chen po de'i nang nas byang chub sems dpa'i kha cig 'di snyam du sems te/
1b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing ni ji tsam zhig du bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom 
1b4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang 'byung ba rnam par dag pa ni ji tsam pa zhig du bsam gyis mi khyag/sangs rgyas 
1b5(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gdung ni ji tsam zhig du bsams gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas 
1b6(phal chen): bcom ldana'adas rnams kyis de bzhin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
1b7(phal chen): 'das rnams kyis rgyas skye ba ni ji tsam zhig du bsamagyis mi khyab//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi de bzhin 
1b8(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sku ni ji tsam zhig du bsamagyes mi khyab//sangs rgyas 
1b9(phal chen): gsung ni ji tsam zhig du bas ma gyis mi khyab//sangs rgyas ma bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
2a1(phal chen): ji tsam zhig du bsam gyis mi khyab//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba ni ji tsam zhig du bsam gyis ma khyab//sangs rgyas 
2a2(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis chags pa mi mnga' ba'i rnam par spyod pa ni ji tsam zhig du bsams gyis mi khyab sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa ni ji tsam zhig du bas ma gyis me 
2a3(phal chen): khyab snyam pa dang /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi sems mkhyen pas thugs su chud nas/byang chub sems dpa' ud dpa' la'i snying po/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs 
2a4(phal chen): kyis byin du brlabs so//sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyis byin dang ldan par mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od kyis snang ba mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi gzi brjid kyis yongs su rgyas par mdzad do//
2a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi mi 'jigs pa la rab tu bzhag go/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa khong du chud par mdzad do//sangs rgyas gyi bdag nyid che ba nyid dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi yul rab tu rtogs par 
2a6(phal chen): mdzad do//chags pa med pa'i rgyud 'byam klas pa la rab tu bzhag go//sangs rgyas thams cad gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa'i rigs rab tu rtogs par mdzad do//sangs rgya kyi tshul gyi 
2a7(phal chen): gnas brjod du med pa la rab tu gzhag pa mdzad do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' u pa la'i snying pos chos kyi dbyings la 'jug rig cing khong du chud pas chags pa med pa'i dbyings su bsgrubs pa'i 
2a8(phal chen): yid dang /chos thams cad la bsgribs pa med par gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang ldan pa'i yang dag par 'jug pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su song ba'i sangs 
2b1(phal chen): rgyas pa lta ba dang/snying pos lta ba'i sems can thams cad yongs su spyod pa'i go ca chen po dang/byang chub sems dpa'i yi dam dang/spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor rgyun 'chad pa med pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi ye shes 
2b2(phal chen): mngon par bsgrub pa skad cig gi cha shas pa rnam par spyod pa dang/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sgo mthong ba mi zad pa dang/so so yang dag par rig pa'i rnam pa gzungs thams cad kyis snang bar byas pa dang/ldan pas 
2b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi mthus byang chub sems dpa' pad mo'i snying po la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/tshad med pa la rnam par spyod pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras /
2b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni rnam par 'phrul ba chen po la rnam par spyod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa thams cad la/sku'i mtshan 
2b5(phal chen): thams cad rab tu brnyas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi 'khor la 'khor ba ni ting nge 'dzin dang/dgongs pas sbyar ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan:'das rnams kyi so so yang 
2b6(phal chen): dag par rig pa bzhin brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa shin tu bstan pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
2b7(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasag rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi gsung dang dbyangs ni tshad med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom
2b8(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba ni thams cad mkho par mkhyen pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis chos kyi dbyangs thugs su chud pa ni brjod du med pa'o//sangs
3a1(phal chen): @#//bstan pa ni bsgribs pa med pa'i mthar thug pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras/bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi tshong /
3a2(phal chen): rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sku gzugs kyi rnam par dag pa na rnyog pa med par rgyud thams cad du byung bas mtha' dang dbus med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
3a3(phal chen): kyi chags pa med pa'i spyan ni chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi spyan mngon par sangs rgyas pas mtha' dang dbus med pa 'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi chags pa med pa'i snyan ni 'gro ba thams cad
3a4(phal chen): kyi sgra bsgribs pa med par thugs su chud pas mtha' dang dbus med
3a5(phal chen): pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom la 'das rnams kyi shangs kyi skye mched ni sangs rgyas kyi pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pas mtha' dang dbus mid pa'o//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi ljags
3a6(phal chen): shin tu yangs pa ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad du gsung gi dbyangs rjes su 'gro bas mtha' dang dbus medapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi sku'i chos nyid ni sems can thams cad ma lus pa'i
3a7(phal chen): sems kyi rnam pa la de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba rnam par dmigs pas mtha' dang dbus  med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das rnams kyi sku dang tha mi dad cing dus gsum chags pa med par thugs
3a8(phal chen): su chud cing rnam par shes pa dang rnam par dag pas mtha' dang dbus me dpa'o//sangs rgyas bcom lta 'das rnams kyi rnam par thar pa bsgribs pa mi mnga' ba la rnam par spyod pa ni sangs rgyas kyi go
3b1(phal chen): 'phang dang rnam par 'phrul pa dang /cho 'phrul mi zad pa yang dag par ston pas mtha' dang dbus med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rnam par dag pa ni brjed 
3b2(phal chen): du med pa'i rgyan byin kyis brlabs pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing nye bar nyon mongs pa med pa yang dag par bstan te/bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dmigs par mdzad pas mtha' dang dbus medapa'o//sangs 
3b3(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i khyad par dang /smon lam thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa ni rnam par rol pa'i ye shes kyi chos yongs su rdzogs te/chos thams cad rdzogs par
3b4(phal chen): byang chub bar bya ba la mtha' dang dbus med pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa'i sangs rgyas
3b5(phal chen): kyi chos rnams so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi skad cig la ye shes mngon par bsgrubs pa yang dag par bstan pa'o//bcu gang zhe na/
3b6(phal chen): skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du dga' ldan gyi gnas nas 'pho ba yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rtan gyi khams thams cad dubyang chub sems dpa'i che ba yongs su bstan pa
3b7(phal chen): dang /skad cig gcig la'ajig rten gyi khams thamasacad du mngon par 'byung ba yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jigs rten gyi khams thams cad da byang chub kyi shing drung du bzhugs shing mngon par
3b8(phal chen): 'tshang rgya ba yongs su bstan pa dang//skad cig gcig la 'jig rjen gyi khams thams cad duchos kyi 'khor lo skor ba yongs su bstan pa dang
4a1(phal chen): @#//'dul ba'i sems can yongs su smin pa'i tshul ji lta ba bzhin du/dus la dbab pa shes pas sangs rgyas bsnyen bkur ba yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams 
4a2(phal chen): thams cad du sems ji ltar 'dul ba'i tshul bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa brjod du med pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi sku sna tshogs rnam par dmigs pa yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jigs rten gyi 
4a3(phal chen): khams thams cad du/rgyan sna tshogs dang rgyan brjod du med pas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba dang/thams cad mkhyen pa'i mdzod yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jig
4a4(phal chen): rten gyi khams thams cad du rnam par dag pa gcig ma yin na/thams cad yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jig ten gyi khams thams cad du'adas pa dang/ma byon pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i sangs 
4a5(phal chen): rgyas skye ba thams cad du dbang po dang/brtson 'grus rnam pa tha dad pa byung ba dang/mos pa dang/spyod pa rnam pa tha dad pa byung pa las dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi rgyud mngon par 'tshang rgya ba yongs su bstan pa ste/
4a6(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi skad cig la ye shes mngon par bsgrub pa yang dag par bstan pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas 
4a7(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sangs rgyas kyi dus las mi 'da' ba ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi mngon par 'tshang rgya ba ni/de'i dus las mi mnga' bas dus la bab pa yin/
4a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyis dge ba rnam par smin pa dang/rnam par smin pa mnyam pa nyid ni de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus la bab pa yino//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyis sems 
4b1(phal chen): can gyi bsam pa yongs su smin pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams lung bstan pa ni de'i dus la pab pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sems can gyi bsam pa'i rgyud du/cho 'phrul yongs su bstan pa ni de'i 
4b2(phal chen): dus la mi 'da' bas dus la bab pa yin//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyis ses can mos pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa'i tshul bzhin du sku yongs su bstan pa de ni de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus la babs pa yin/sangs rgyas 
4b3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams btang snyoms su bzhugs pa ni de'i dus las mi 'das bas dus la bab pa yin no//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams yul dang /grong dang /grong rdal du gshegs pa de ni de'i dus las ni 'da' bas 
4b4(phal chen): dus la bab pa yin//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam dad pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa tshul bzhin du sems can yongs su 'dzin pa na/de'i dus las mi 'da' ba dus las pab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
4b5(phal chen): rnams sems can kun nas nyon mongs pa dang ldan zhing gdul dka' ba la gzhan la ltos pa mi mnga' bar 'dul ba ni de'i dus las mi 'ad bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas 
4b6(phal chen): kyi rnam par 'phrul ba bsam gyis mi khyab pas/rnam par 'phrul ba bstan pa ni de'i dus las mi 'ga' bas dus la bab pa yin//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
4b7(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi dus las mi 'da' ba'o//skye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sags rgyas kyi yul dbe med cing bsam gyis mi khyab pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/'jig rten gyi 
4b8(phal chen): khams thams cad ma lus par skyil mo krung gcig gis rgyas par dgang ba dang /dus cig tu sangs rgyas thams cad yongs su bstan pa dang /'od gzer gcig gis 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad snang 
5a1(phal chen): @#//par bya ba dang/sku gzugs gcig gis lus dang/gzugs thams cad yongs su bstan pa dang /gnas gcig na bzhugs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ma lus par byon pa'i skur 'gyur 
5a2(phal chen): ba dang|ye shes gcig rtogs pas chos thams cad la bsgribs pa med par 'gyur ba dang/thugs gcig bskyed pas chos kyi dbyings thams cad ma lus par rgyas par 'gengs pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi che ba nyid tshad 
5a3(phal chen): med pa dang ldan pa'i gshegs stabs gcig gis de kho na nyid yang dag par bstan pa dang/dus gsum thams cad sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang gis rgyas par 'gengs pa dang/'gro ba thams cad dang ma 'dres par bzhugs 
5a4(phal chen): shing 'das pa dang/ma byon pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang/mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis rgyud kyi tshul gcig pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'angs 
5a5(phal chen): rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi yul dpe med cing bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes te/bcu gang zhe na/
5a6(phal chen): 'jug pa med pa'i chos thams cad la 'jug pas/smon lam mngon par bsgrub pa'i ye shes dang/lus med pa la lus mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shas dang/chos thams cad mi gnyis pa la mngon par 'tshang rgya 
5a7(phal chen): ba mngon par sgrub pa'i ye she sa dang/sems can gyi mthar thug par bdag med pa la sems can 'dul ba mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shis dang /chos thams cad mtshan ma sna tshogs mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes dang /
5a8(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams kyi yul ma skyes pa la 'jig rten gyi khams 'du ba dang/'jig pa mngon par sgrub pa'i ye se sa dang/yul thams cad mngon par 'du bya ba med pa la/las kyi rnam par smin pa mngon par sgrub
5b1(phal chen): pa'i ye shes dang/chos thams cad brjod du med pa la/chos kyi dbyings su gsung mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes dang/chos tham sa cad kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang bas 'jug pa med pa la kun nas 
5b2(phal chen): nyon mongs pa dang/rnam par byang ba mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shas dang/chos thams cad ma skyed pa la skye ba dang/'jig pa'i mtshan nyid rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba mngon par sgrub pa'i yi shes te/kye rgyal ba'i 
5b3(phal chen): sras bcu po de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shis so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshad med pa'i khongs su chud pa dag ste/bcu 
5b4(phal chen): gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sku ni dus gsum thams cad kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i khongs su chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan dang rnams sems can thams cad 'dul ba mdzad pa ni/
5b5(phal chen): cho 'phrul chen po gsum gyi khongs su chud pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams tshig rab tu dbye ba thams cad la mkhas pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi gzungs thams cad kyi khongs su chud pa yin/ sangs rgyas 
5b6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis chos kyi 'khor lo thams cad ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i so so yang dag par rig pa bzhir khongs su chud pa yin/snangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dang rnams sems can tham sa cad yongs su mi tong 
5b7(phal chen): ba ni thugs rje chen po'i khongs su chud pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams rtag tu mnyam par gzhag pa ni dus thams cad du sems can thams cad la rnam par gzigs pa'i khams su chud pa yin/
5b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba ni sems can rnam par 'dus pa thams cad khongs su chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyi chags pa mi mnga' bar spyod pa ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad 
6a1(phal chen): @#//khong du chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi skad cig gcig tu ni 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas skye ba thams cad khongs su chud pa yin//sangs 
6a2(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi nyi ma gcig tu ni dus gsum gyi bskal pa'i grangs khong du chud par bstan pa thams cad khongs su chud ba yin//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
6a3(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi tshad med pa'i khongs su chud pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcud po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/bdud 
6a4(phal chen): kyi dkrugs ma rab tu 'joms zhing rnam par 'thob pa ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'o chos bzod par bka' ba'o//mu stegs can gzhan dang /phas kyi rgol ba tshar gcad pa ni sangs 
6a5(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnamasakyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chosabazod par bka' ba'o/'dul ba'i sems can thams cad rab tu tshim par mdzad pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adasarnams kyi shin tu rgya cheba'i chos bzod par
6a6(phal chen): dka' ba'o//sems can 'dul ba'i dbang gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du gshegs pa ni sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgyache ba'i chos bzod par bka' pa'o//chos kyi dbyings thugs su chud pa'i
6a7(phal chen): shes ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'idas rnams kyi shin tu rgya chen pa'i chos bzod par dka'aba'o//'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad ma lus par skus khyab cing sangs rgyas kyi sku rnam par phye pa sna tshogs thams cad
6a8(phal chen): du mi gnyis pa ni/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi shin du rgya chen po'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//gsung gi yan lag thams cad la sems can mos par bya ba dang /thos par bya ba dang /gdul bar bya ba don yod
6b1(phal chen): yod par so so yang dag par rig pa thogs pa med pas bstan pa'i tshul du so so yang dag par rigs pa bzhi 'byung ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//sangs 
6b2(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi ba spu'i bu ga gcig nas de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed bsam gyis mi khyab cing /thogs pa med pa'i tshul du 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad kyi 
6b3(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi thabs kyis 'byung zhing de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi sku'i ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas 'byung ba ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba chos bzod par dka' pa'o//de bzhin 
6b4(phal chen): gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdul shin tu phra ba cig la sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'jig rten gyi khams thamscad kyi rdul shin tu phra bsnyed yongs su ston cing /sangs rgyas kyi rgyan dang rang 
6b5(phal chen): bzhin gyi lhab lhub dang /chos kyi 'khor lo skor brgyun mi 'chad cing sems can rnam par 'dul ba thogs pa med pa 'jig rten gyi khams de dag tu gtogs pa rnams la'ang mi bde ba dang /gnod pa med kyang rdul 
6b6(phal chen): shin tu phra ba de'ang yangs par ma byas/'jig rten gyi khams de dag/kyang chud par ma byas par ji ltar gnas pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyang thugs su chud pa'i ye shes mnga' ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
6b7(phal chen): rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//mun pa med pa'i khams de bzhin gshegs pas mngon par sangs rgyas te/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la mun pa med cing bsgribs pa mi mnga' 
6b8(phal chen): la sems can pha rol gyi bsgribs pa sna tshogs kyi gnas rnam par sel na/de la de bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang ldan pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams shin tu rgya che ba'i chos 
7a1(phal chen): @#//bzod par bka' ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas 
7a2(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis bdag nyid che ba rnams thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu dag pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi yang dag par bsgrubs pa bdag nyid che ba ni sngon gyi mtha' 
7a3(phal chen): nas rnam par thams cad du kha na ma thob med cing rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi rgyud du rigs dang ldan par 
7a4(phal chen): 'byung ba'i bdag nyid che ba ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rnam par dag pas/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma thong ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni rnam par shes 
7a5(phal chen): pa la mi gnas pa ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug par gnas pas/rnam pa thams cad de kha na ma tho ba med cing sha ni rnam par dag pa'o//sang
7a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni dus gsum la mi gnas par rnam par spyod cing chos thams cad la rten par yang dag par bcom pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi bdag nyid cheb ni/rnam pa thams 
7a7(phal chen): cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnams par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni dngos po rnam pa tha dad pa la rnam par gzhag pa gcig gis len pa med pas sangs rgyas bcom ldang 'das rnams kyi bdag 
7a8(phal chen): nyid che ba ni rnam pa thams cad du kha nam tho ba med cing shin turnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams na sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mthar chos kyi dbyings la gnas pas/mnyam pa'i bsod 
7b1(phal chen): nams mi zad pa dang ldan pas mi zad pa'i bsod nams mnga' ba ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni rnam par thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin du rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
7b2(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sku gzugs dang kha dog mtha' yas pas/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad na sems can 'dul ba'i dus dang dbang gis chags pa med par bzhugs pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
7b3(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid tshe ba ni rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma 'tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni mi 'jigs pa bzhi brnyes pas bag tsha ba mi mnga' bar bzhugs shing /khyu mchog dang seng ge'i 
7b4(phal chen): nga ro 'byin te rgol ba'i nang na stag lta bu mthu dang ldan pa la chos thams cad bstan pa la thugs sgron ma lta bu mnga' bas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi bdag nyid che pa ni rnam pa thams cad du kho na ma tho ba med cing shin1 tu 
7b5(phal chen): rnam par dag pa'o//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das nas bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa zhig lon yang mtshan kyi tshig thos pa tsam gyis bzhugs pa dang 'dra bar sems 
7b6(phal chen): can rnams kyi yon tan shin tu spyod zhing mya ngan las 'das nas/de srid kyi yun ring po 'das kyang sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i yon tan zad par mi 'gyur bas//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi bdag nyid che ba ni rnam pa thams cad ca
7b7(phal chen): kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med pa'i yang rjod du med pa dag na bzhugs shing 'jig rten gyi khams rgyang ring po na bzhugs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams 'di 
7b8(phal chen): sems can rnams kyis rjes su dran par byas nas sku mngon par ston pa 'dzin pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni rnamapa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shan tu rnam par dag pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag 
8a1(phal chen): @#//ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
8a2(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi shin tu rnam par dag pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni/sangs rgyas kyi tshangs pa'i spyod pa rnam par dag pa la sngon gyi smon lam gyis shin tu rnam par dag 
8a3(phal chen): pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni tshogs pa med pa'i tshul khrims 
8a4(phal chen): kyis shin tu rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa sel bas shin tu rnam par dag yin/sangs rgyas bcom 
8a5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing gis shin tu rnam 
8a6(phal chen): par dag pa yin/sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'khor kyis shin tu rnam par dag ba yin/sangs rgyas bcom dam 'das rnams ni rigs rnam par dag pas shin tu rnam par dag pe yin/sangs rgyas bcom lan 'das rnams ni 
8a7(phal chen): gzugs kyi sku'i mtshan dang /dpe byad bzang po rnam par dag pas shin du rnam par dag pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom
8a8(phal chen): mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi phung po bsgribs pa med pas shin tu rnam par dang pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom
8a9(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sang rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa thams cad la mthu chen po brnyes shing /pha rol tu phyin pa dam pa brnyes pas shin tu
8a10(phal chen): rnam par dag pa yin te//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras btsu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom
8a11(phal chen): dus thams cad du gnas pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa thob pa'i sems can rnams la dus thams cad du sangs rgyas bstan pa dang sems can sems can shes dang ldan pa rnams la/dus thams cad du chos bstan pa dang /sems
8b1(phal chen): can sangs rgyas la dad pa'i sems dang ldan pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba dus thams cad du skyed pa dang /chos kyi skyon med par 'das pa'i sems can rnams dus thams cad du ye shes khong du chud par bya ba dang /'gro ba thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi 
8b2(phal chen): mdzad pa dus thams cad du rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du dus thams cad du/chags pa ming mnga' bar gshegs pa dang /sems can thams cad la thugs rje chen po cas 
8b3(phal chen): thams cad du mi gtong ba dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa dus thams cad du thogs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgya kyi rnam par 'phrul 
8b4(phal chen): pa sna tshogs dus thams cad du thogs med pa dang /sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams chos kyi dbyings gnas pa'i sangs rgyas dus thams cad 'byung ba ste//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs 
8b5(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi mdzad pa 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad du gnas pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcwu po 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs 
8b6(phal chen): rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i tshul rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan das rnams ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i skus ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/
8b7(phal chen): rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi phrin las tshad med pa brnyes pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zadapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldang 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi yul mnga' bas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zadaba'o//sangs rgyas 
8b8(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni dge ba'i rtsa ba tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa mnga' bas ye shes rgya ma tsho ma zid pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni chos kyi char mngon par 'babs pas ye shes rgya mtsho
9a1(phal chen): @#//mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang legs pa mang po mnga' bas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams
9a2(phal chen): ni sngon gyi mtha'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i smon lam dang khyad par mnga' bas ye shes rgya ma tsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams na phyi ma'i mtha'i mur 'gro ba thams cad sangs rgyas kyi phrin las
9a3(phal chen): rgyun mi chad par bsgrubs pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zadapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni sems can gyi rigs thams cad ma lus pa'i sems dang spyod pa la ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad ba'o//sangs 
9a4(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i bsod nams zil gyis mi non pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi bzad pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs
9a5(phal chen): pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad po'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras pa bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul rnams dang /bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste pha rol tu phyin
9a6(phal chen): pa thams cad kyis mngon par 'byung ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste chos thams cad la mi mkhyen pa gang yang mi mkha' pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi
9a7(phal chen): tshul yin no//'di lta ste thams cad kyang thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin na//'di lta ste thams cadakyang stobs bcu tshad med pa dang ldan pa 'di ni
9a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi tshul yin no//'i lta ste thams cad kyang bla na med pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba 'di ni sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi tshul yin no//'di lta s+he thams cad
9b1(phal chen): kyang sems can 'dul ba la yang dag par zhugs pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste thams cad kyang 'gro ba thams cad kyi don du mngon par sangs rgyas pa 'di ni sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 
9b2(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi tshul yin no //'di lta ste 'dul ba'i sems can rnams 'dul ba la thogs pa mi mnga' ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom lan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin//'di lta ste mi gnyis par yang dag par spyod pa'i mtshan 
9b3(phal chen): nyid kyis chos la rab tu gzhol pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcos ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yon no//'di lta ste ji ltar yongs su smin par gyur pa dang /dul bar gyur pa'i sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi yul mu med 
9b4(phal chen): pas sangs rgyas kyi yongs su mya ngan las 'da' pa ston pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das kyi tshul yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul lo//kye rgyal 
9b5(phal chen): ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi chos mtha' yas pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgo rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can gyi khams shin tu bstan pa'i 
9b6(phal chen): sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can gyi spyod pa sna tshogs shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni sems can thams cad 
9b7(phal chen): kyi las dang rnam par smin pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgor ma tha' yas pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasam rnams ni sems can thams cad la gdams ngag dang man ngag shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtho' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas
9b8(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams nisems can mtha' yas pa rnam par spyod pa'i nan tan gyi sgor mtha' yas pa'o/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
10a1(phal chen): @#//su gzhog pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni byang chub sems dpa' khyad par gyi smon lam rnam pa tha dad pas 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs 
10a2(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasani 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ma lus pa'i 'du pa dang 'jig pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgor ma tha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa ni byang 
10a3(phal chen): chub sems dpa'i bsam pa dang mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs su bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yasapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du 'das pa dang /ma byon 
10a4(phal chen): pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa ni bskal pa yangs pa de dang de dag du bstan cing brjod pa dang /yongs su bshad pas/sangs rgyas kyi ye shes thams cad khong du chud par 
10a5(phal chen): bya ba'i sgor mtha' yas pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos mtha' yas pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgo'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni 
10a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sems can rnams la sgrub pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi sku yongs su bstan pa ni sems can rnams 
10a7(phal chen): la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasrnams kyi gsung yang sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin la sgrub po//sangs rgyas bco ldan 'das rnams len pa med pa yang sems 
10a8(phal chen): can rnams la sangs rgyas gyi phrin las sgrub po//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam len pa mi mdzad pa yang sems can rnams la/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrub po//sa dang chu dang me dang /rlung gis kyang sangs rgyas 
10b1(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni byin kyis rlabs kyi yul dang mi dmigs pa thams cad yul du mdzad pas kyang sems 
10b2(phal chen): can rnams la sangs rgyas gyi phrin las sgrub po//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi mtshan kyang sems can rnams la sang rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni zhing la 
10b3(phal chen): dmigs par mdzad pas kyang sems can rnams las sangs rgyas kyi phrin lasasgrub po//sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pas kyang sems can rnam la sangs rgyas 
10b4(phal chen): kyi phrin las sgrub po/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams mi gsung bar bzhugs pa yang sems can rnams la sa sangs rgyas kyi phrin la sgrub ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas
10b5(phal chen): kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin lasasems can rnams la sgrub pa'o////@#//bam po gnyis pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcupo 'di dag
10b6(phal chen): ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos te/bcu gang zhe na/smon lam chen po la rab tu brtan zhing smon lam zhig pa mi mnga' bas ji skad du gsung ba de ltar mdzad cing gnyis su mi 
10b7(phal chen): gsung ba 'di ni sems can gyi gtso bo'i tshes dang po'o//phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal pa thams cad du byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la yon tan thams cad yongs su rdzogs par mdzad pas rab du brtson zhing skyid
10b8(phal chen): lug pa mi mnga' ba 'di ni sems can gyis gtso bo'i chos gnyis pa'o//sems can 'dul bar 'gyur ba gcig gcig gzigs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med par gshegs shing 'jig rten
11a1(phal chen): @#//gyi khams thams cad du gshegs pa la thags thogs med pa 'di ni sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos gsum pa'o//zhe sdang ba'i sems can rnams la thugs rje chen pos snyoms pa dang /dad pa'i sems 
11a2(phal chen): can rnams la thugs rje chenapos snyoms pa 'di ni sems can gyi btso bo'i chos bzhi pa'o//gang gi tshe bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par zhugs nas/bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs 
11a3(phal chen): pa'i byang chub tu mngon par sangs rgyas kyi bar du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas su mi 'gyuro ro//snyam du byang chub kyi sems mnyams pa 'di ni sems canagyi gtso bo'i chos 
11a4(phal chen): lnga pa'o//dag pa'i rtsa ba thams cad yang dag par bsgrub cing /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su sngo bas 'jug gi/'jig rten pa'i rgyud la ma yin pa 'di ni sems can gyi go bo'i chos drug pa'o//sangs 
11a5(phal chen): rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su sngon sangs rgyas gyis spyad pa'i sems su lus kyi las dang /ngag gi las dang /yid kyi las kyis slob cing nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi spyod pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid duyongs 
11a6(phal chen): su sngo ba med pas/bla na med pa yang dgapar rdzogs pa'i byang chub du mngon par sangs rgyas pa 'di ni sems can gyigatso bo'i chos bdun pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i sems rnam par dag cing 'od gsal ba dang /thams 
11a7(phal chen): cad du snang zhing kun tu 'od 'byung ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la snang zhing thams cad du snyoms pas/thams cad mkhyen pa nying yongs su rdzogs par byed pa 'di ni sems can gyis gtso bo'i chos brgyad pa'o//
11a8(phal chen): 'jig rten pa'i bde ba thams cad yongs su spangs te/'jig rten pa'i dga' ba thams cad la ma chags pa dang /'jig rten thams cad kyis gos pa med pa'i smon lam thams cad du mnyam pa nyid kyis/'jig rten thams 
11b1(phal chen): cad bde ba thams cad dang ldan par byas te/sdug bsngal dang spros pa thams cad rab tu 'joms pa 'di ni sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos dgu pa'o//sdug bsngal gyi mi bde ba thams cad nyams su bab ste/'gro ba thams 
11b2(phal chen): cad kyi don du dka' thub gyis shin tu gdungs kyang blang bar sems bskyed pas/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs kyi ngas su srung ba dang ldan pa dang /stobs bcu'i sa'i bar du sangs rgyas kyi yul la mos pas mthar thug pa 
11b3(phal chen): 'di ni sems can gyi gtso ba'i chos bcu pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sems can gyi gtso bo'i cho sa so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'i dag ni sangs rgyas 
11b4(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chags pa med pa rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du gshegs pas chags pa 
11b5(phal chen): mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan las rnams ni 'jig rten gyi kham ma lus pa thams cad ni bzhugs pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyodapa yin/sangs rgyas bcom 
11b6(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni 'jig rten gyis khams thams cad na bzhugs pa dang bzhengs pa dang /bzhud pa chags pa mi mnga' bar rnams par spyod pa yin/sangs 
11b7(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam par spyod pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni 'jig rten gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha' dang /da ltar byung ba'i mtha' la chags pa mi mnga' bar rnal par 
12a1(phal chen): @#//spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad tsam gyi 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor na bzhugs kyang chags pa mi mnga' bar spyod pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom 
12a2(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sems can ma lus pa thams cad kyi sems dang spyod pa la gzigs shing /thugs kyi skad gcig cig la chos 'phrul chen po gsum gyis sems can rnam par 'dul yang chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par 
12a3(phal chen): spyod pa yin//sangs rgyas gcom ldan 'das rnams ni sku gcig gis kyang tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chen por rnam par spyod kyang chags pa mi mnga' par rnam par 
12a4(phal chen): spyod pa yin //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can gyi khams 'byam klas pa ma lus pa thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos ma lus pa thams cad gyi mthar thug pa bsam gyis mi khyab par ston cing 
12a5(phal chen): gsung yang chags pa mi mnga' par ranam par spyod pa yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chags pa med pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras 
12a6(phal chen): bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa rnams te//bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi sku mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po sna tshogs kyis rnam 
12a7(phal chen): par brgyan pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi sku'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla named pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi gsung yan lag gi mchog dam pa drug bcu dang ldan zhing yan 
12a8(phal chen): lag lnga brgyas yongs su bzang ba dang /rgyan brgya stong du mas rab tu brgyan pa dang /dbyangs kyi yan lag mtha' yas mu med pas rnam par dag pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi don dang tshig dang yi ge brjod du med pa'i 
12b1(phal chen): yang brjod du med pas rab tu tshim par mdzad pa dang/bag tsha ba mi mda' bar seng ge'i nga ro chen po sgrogs pa dang/'jigs pa med pa chen pos bsnyengs pa mi mnga' bar gyur pa dang/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa tham+sa
12b2(phal chen): cad du sems can thams cad la grag cing stsal te/khong du chud par 'gyur ba dang/sems can bsam pa tha dad pa dang/dbang po tha dad pa rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul bar nye bar gnas pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan
12b3(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi gsung gi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi thugs kyi phrin las ni stobs bcus brgyan pa sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin chen po thams cad kyi me tog shin tu rgyas pa
12b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bcwo brgyad kyi rgyan kyis shin tu brgyan cing bsgrubs pas chags pa med pa'i spyod yul dang/chos kyi dbyings kyi gzhi la rab tu rten pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gting med pa rab tu brnyes
12b5(phal chen): pa༐ chos kyi dbyings ma lus pathams cad kyi rgyan du gyur pa/dus gsum kyi chos kyi dbyings ma lus pathams cad kyi sems can kun gyi sems dang spyod pa thams cad skad cig gcig dang ldan pa'i shes rab kyis rab tu
12b6(phal chen): mkhyen pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das rnams kyi dgongs pa'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla named pa'o//sangs rgyas bco ma ldan 'das rnams ni 'od zer bye ba brjod du med pa rab tu 'gyed par mdzad de/'od
12b7(phal chen): zer de dag re re la yang 'od zer gyi 'khor kyi dra ba brjod du med pa dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad ma lus par byed cing 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud thams cad mun pa med par ston pa/sangs
12b8(phal chen): rgyas skye ba dang/mi 'bral bas/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las phyir mi ldog cing don yod pa/sangs rgyas skye ba brjod du med pa rang bzhin rnam par dag cing kun tu snyoms par 'byung ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas

13a6(phal chen): 'phags pa/'jig rten thams chad las 'das pa'i chos kyis yang dag par spyod pa/tshig gi lam dang bral ba/brjod du med pa'i mthar phyin pa/phung po dang khams dang /skye mched bral ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas

13b1(phal chen): gyur pa/'di thams cad kyi 'byung gnas su gyur pa ste//'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni kha dog mtha' yas pa mnga' ba ste/

13b3(phal chen): kha dog gzhan khams gsum du gtogs pa thams cad zil kyis gnon pa'i kha dog mnga' ba'o//kha dog thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni kha dog bla na med pa 
13b4(phal chen): mnga' ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi kha dog gyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla named pa'o//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams ni dge bas shin tu rnam par dag pa ste/'das pa dang ma byon pa dang 
13b5(phal chen): da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs dge ba rin po che'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa la shin tu byung ba'o//chos nyid kyi rnam pa thams cad du khyad par yod pa dang khyad par med pa'i spyod pas rnam par dag 
13b6(phal chen): pa'i rgyan kyis ye shes skye ba thams cad kyi nang du gtogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs bsgribs pa med pa'i rgyud rnam par dag pas/'jig rten gyi mi snyan pa thams cad kyis mi snyan par brjod du med pa ste/

13b8(phal chen): rgyan mnga' ba ste/shin tu dad pas yongs su rtog pa'i rnyog pa mi mnga' ba sku'i phrin las yongs su bstsal pa lta bas mi skyo ba/dgongs pa shin tu rnam par stsal ba thugs rje chen po mnga' ba/'gro ba thams cad skyob pa

14a6(phal chen): lus par yongs su brjod pas mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' sgyur ba'i chos dang po'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems

14b1(phal chen): dang ldan pa nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mtha' sa gtugs pa thams cad gcig nas gcig tu 'bang pa dang /gcig nas gcig tu bsdu ba dang /cig nas gcig tu bsre bas 'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dang /gzhan dag gzhan 
14b2(phal chen): gyi phyogs su rab tu bzhag kyang sems can gyi khams rnams 'khor zhing rmongs par mi 'gyur la/the tsom du mi 'gyur bar mdzad pas mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' sgyur ba'i chos gsum pa'o//
14b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad byin gyis rlob pa'i ye shes kyis 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyan phan tshun du byin gyis brlab pa dang /dgongs pa'i skad gcig gis 'jig rten gyi 
14b4(phal chen): khams thams cad kyi rgyan yang dag par bstan pa dang /bskal pa grangs med par brjod kyang yi ge shin tu bstan pa zad mi shes pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams ma lusapa thams cad rgyan thams cad kyis rnam par brgyan pa 
14b5(phal chen): dang /mi mthun pa med cing nye bar nyon mongs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gcig tu yongs su 'du bar sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa thams cad byin gyis brlabs pa la mnga' che ba ste/'di ni 
14b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pas mnga' chen sgyur ba'i chos bzhi pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sangs rgyas kyis 'dul ba'i sems can gzigs na sems can gcig gi don

18a8(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi/sems can gdul ba rnam par dmigs pa la/rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs tshad med mu med par mngon par bsgrub pa mdzad pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi thabs 
18b1(phal chen): mkhas pa lnga pa'o//chos thams cad lta ba med pas tha mi dad pa dang bye brag med pa dang /'byung ba med pa dang mtshan nyid med pa dang /sna tshogs med pa dang /blang ba med pa dang /dor ba med pa dang /rang bzhin 
18b2(phal chen): med par rab tu mkhyen kyang /chos kyi rgyun sha ni tu nges pa yang dag par bsgrub cing /'jig rten gyi tshul dang 'gal ba med pa dang /rang bzhIn med pa yang dag par gzigs pas thams cad mkhyen pa/
18b3(phal chen): thams cad gzigs pa/mkhyen par bya ba thams cad ma lus par gzigs par chos la mnga' sgyur ba thams cad kyang rab tu ston la/de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin las kyang mi nyam pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom 
18b4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa drug pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni thugs su chud pa'i rnam pa gcig gis gzhan dang gzhan mkhyen par bya ba chos de'i skyon med pa las rnam 
18b5(phal chen): par 'das pa gnas med pa dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par dag pa mnyam pa nyid kyi chos skyon med pas/dus thams cad du gnas pa rab tu mkhyen te/de la yang nyi ma gcig gam/zla ba phyed dam/lo cig gam/lo 
18b6(phal chen): brgya 'am/bskal pa cig pa dang /'du bas rjes su bsnyag pa dang /phyir phyogs pa mi mnga' bar dus tshad med pa dang /nyi ma gcig dang /yud tsam gcig gi dus dang snga dro nas nyi ma phye'i dus dang /phyi 
18b7(phal chen): dro nas snga dro'i dus dang /nyi ma gung nas phyi dro'i dus dang /nyi ma bgrangs pa'i dus dang /nyi ma bdun dang /zla ba phyed kyi dus dang /zla ba dang /lo dang /lo brgya'i dus dang bskal pa gcig gyi dus dang /tshad 
18b8(phal chen): med grangs med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dus nas/phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i dus su chos bstan pa dang /chos kyi 'khor lo thogs pa med cing phyir mi ldog/rgyun 'chad par mi 'gyur/bral bar mi 'gyur/
19a1(phal chen): @#//par chad par mi 'gyur/med par mi 'gyur/'jig par mi 'gyur g.yo bar mi 'gyur/rang du mi 'gyur/'khor de la gnas pa nyid du yang rgyun 'chad par mi 'gyur te/'di ni sangs rgyas 
19a2(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa bdun pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad du rtag tu dus la bab par bzhugs pa ste/sangs rgyas 
19a3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni mi 'jigs pa tshad med mnga' ba'o//so so yang dag par rig pa grangs med pa mnga' ba'o//so so yang ngag par rig pa tshad med par mnga' ba'o//so so yang de dag par rig pa 
19a4(phal chen): zad pa mi shes pa mnga' ba'o//so so yag dag par rig pa thogs pa med pa mnga' ba'o//soso yang dag par rig pa mtha' yas pa mnga' ba'o//thun mong ma yin pa'i so so yang dag par rig mnga' ba'o//
19a5(phal chen): so so yang dag par rig ba mthar thug pa med pa mnga' ba'o/so so yang dag par rig de kho na dang ldan pa mnga' ba'o//so so ya dag par rig pa tshig gi tshul thams cad rab tu rtogs pa mnga' ba'o//chos thams cad so so 
19a6(phal chen): yang dag par rig pa mnga' ba'o//bsam pa dang mos pa dang dbang po ji lta ba bzhin du chos kyi tshul dang nam grangs tshul ji ltar rnam par bkod pa bzhin du sa 'tshams med par ston te/mdo sde bye ba khrag khrig brjod du med 
19a7(phal chen): pa'i yang brjod du med pa rnams mdo sde de dang de la thog ma dang dbus gi tshul gyis yang dag par ston to//'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa brgyad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
19a8(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni ming med kyang ming dang rigs su byung ba'i chos thams cad tshig dang yi ges shin tu dbyangs mngon par sangs rgyas pa ste/ming 'das pa ma yin pa dang /ming ma 'ongs pa ma yin pa dang /mang 
19b1(phal chen): da ltar byung ba ma yin pa dang/ming sems can ma yin pa dang/ming zhing ma yin pa dang/ming yon tan ma yin pa dang/ming byang chub sems dpa' ma yin pa dang/ming sangs rgyas ma yin pa dang/ming grangs ma
19b2(phal chen): yin pa dang/ming grangs med pa ma yin pa dang/ming skye ba ma yin pa dang/minga'agag pa ma yin pa dang/ming med pa ma yin pa dang /ming yod pa ma yin pa dang/mi gcig pa ma yin pa dang ming tha dad pa ma yin pa dang/
19b3(phal chen): ming thams cad ma yin pa dang/chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis brjod du med pa dang /phyogs med pa dang brda' med pa dang /tshig gi lam med pa sgra dang bral ba dang/sgra'i rnam pa thams cad mi 'byung ba/yongs su
19b4(phal chen): bsgrubs pa med pa/byas pa dang rnam par byas pa med pa rnam pa gcig ma yin pa/rnam pa mang po ma yin pa yang thugs su chud la/brjod pa dang spyod pa thams cad la yang pha rol tu phyin pa'i mchog brnyes pa dag
19b5(phal chen): ste|rnam par rtog pa'i yul mi mnga' ba dag go/mngon par 'du mdzad pa med pa la rab tu brtson pa dag go/rtog pa dang kun tu rtog pa yang yongs su rtog pa 'gags pa dag go/spyod pa thams cad la mi gnas

19b7(phal chen): tshor ba'i spros pa med pa dang/'du shes kyi grangs med pa dang/'du byed kun tu rgyu ba med pa dang/rnam par shes pa nye bar len pa med pa dang/rang bzhin gyis zhi ba rab tu mkhyen te/chos thams cad skye mched kyi rgyud med
19b8(phal chen): pa dang/khams rnams mi dmigs pa dang/chos thams cad mi 'jigs pa dang/chos thams can bskyed du med cing/rang bzhin gyis nam mkha' lta bu dang/chos thams cad dben pa dang /las med pa dang/rnam
20a1(phal chen): @#//par smin pa med pa dang /spyod pas 'byung ba med pa dang /ma grub pa dang /ma byung ba dang /grangs kyis mi brjod pa dang yod pa ma yin pa dang /ma skyes pa dang ma 'gags pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa

20a4(phal chen): cad shin tu bstan pa la pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam pa brnyes pa dag ste/nges pa dang ma nges pa dang /log par nges pa'i phung po gsum rnam par dgod pa rnams la stobs bcus thugs su chud pa'i ye shes mi 'jigs pa bzhis

20a6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhasapa'o////shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//bam po gsum pa//kye 
20a7(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas gyi phrin las rgya chen po tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa rnams te/de dag ni sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs ma gtogs 
20a8(phal chen): par lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten dang /'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis 'jug pa mi nus pa'o//bcu gang zhe na/phyogs bcu'i nam mkha'i 
20b1(phal chen): dbyings kyi mtha' sa gtugs pa'i chos kyi dbyings thams cad 'jig rten khams ma lus pa thams cad du dga' ldan du skye ba bstan pa dang/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mu med pa dag la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las
20b2(phal chen): mdzad de/kha dog tshad med pa gzi brjid tshad med pa snang ba tshad med pa/dbyangs tshad med pa dbyangs kyi yan lag rnam par dag pa tshad med pa gsung brjod pas rnam par migs pa tshad med pa/ting nge 'dzin tshad med
20b3(phal chen): pa la spyod pa/shes rab dang ye shes kyi yul tshad med pa mnga' ba'i rnams lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten la phan 'dogs pa mdzad de/bdud dang bcas pa tshangs pa dang bcas pa/dge sbyong dang bcas pa/bram ze'i skye
20b4(phal chen): dgu dang bcas pa/lha dang/mi dang/lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten la 'phan 'dogs pa mdzad do//byams pa chen po'i spyod yul thogs pa med pa dang/thabs thugs rje chen po'i sgo'i mthar thugs pas sems
20b5(phal chen): can la phan 'dogs pa mdzad//bsod nams kyi stobs dang/mnga' phun sum tshogs pa dang/dbang po rnam par spyangs pa dang/cang shes kyi sems yongs su dag pas kyang sems can rnams la phan

20b7(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad gnon pas kyang sems can rnam la phan 'dogs pa mdzadade/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las dang po'o//dga' ldan gyi pho brang nas 'phos pa med kyang
20b8(phal chen): yum gyi skur gshegs shing/byang chub sems dpa'i sku tha ma skye bar ston pa/sgyu ma lta bur yongs su spyod pa/sprul pa lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin kyi mthar thug pa/mig yor lta bur rnam par dmigs pa/nam
21a1(phal chen): @#//mkha'i yul tshad med par khyab pa/bzhed pa bzhin du skye ba yang chags pa mi mnga' ba smig sgyu ba lta bur bstan pa/nyon mongs pa med pa'i chos la yang dag par gzhol ba/gnas med pa'i ye shes 
21a2(phal chen): la gnas pa/rang bzhin gyis 'dod chags dang bral bas rnam par dag pa/rgyan shin tu rgya che ba'i mdzod kyis sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//khang pa brtsegs pa rin po che las byas pa'i pho brang chen po rin po che'i rgyan 
21a3(phal chen): mang po dang ldan pa na bzhugs pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las badzad do//rdzu 'phrul gyis kyang sangs rgyas kyi phring las mdzad do//yid la bya ba'i byin gyi rlabs chen pos kyang sangs rgyas kyi phri las mdzad do//nam 
21a4(phal chen): par 'phrul pa'i mdzod chen po yang dag par bstan pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad do//ye shes kyi nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen pos kyang sangs rgyas gyi phrin las mdzad do/yum sku skyed pa'i sku na bzhugs 
21a5(phal chen): pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phreng las mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi yul gyi spyod yul chen po brnyes pas kyang sangs rgyas kyis phrin las mdzad do/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis 'jig rten byin gyis brlabs pa mtha' yas 
21a6(phal chen): pas sangs rgyas kyi phrin la mdzad do//ting nge 'dzin chen po grangs med pa la rnam par spyod cing /snyoms par 'jug pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi prin las mdzad do/ting nge 'dzin las bzhengs pa de dang de dag gis 
21a7(phal chen): kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do/spyod pa'i thog ma la yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa chen po'i mthar thug pa'i bar du thog ma dang dbus dang tha ma'i tshul gyis sangs rgyas kyi phrin las badzad do//sa 'di la 
21a8(phal chen): gnyis su med pas thams cad mthar phyin par byas nas tha mar 'jig rten gyi grangs kyis skye ba yongs su ston te/skye bas kyang 'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las kyi mdzad do/gzhon nur gyur pas 
21b1(phal chen): kyang 'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//byang chub sems dpar gyur pas kyang 'jig rten thams cad su sangs rgyas kyi phrin las badzad do//mngon par 'byung bas kyang 'jig rten thams
21b2(phal chen): cad du sangs rgyas kyi phri las mdzad do//mngon par 'tshang rgya bas kyang 'jig rten thams can du sangs rgyas kyi phrin bas mdzad do//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bas kyang 'jig rten thamasacad du sangs
21b3(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de//thabs mkhas pa sna tshogs kyis kyang 'jig rten sna tshogs 'jig rten tshad med pa/'jig rten kun tu mtha' yas pa phyogs kyi dra ba'i rgyud dang/ras thams cad kyi 'jig rten ma lus
21b4(phal chen): pa thams cad du sangs rgyas gyi phrin las mdzad do//zhing gi rgyud ma lus pa'i 'jig rten du sangs rgyas kyi phris las mdzad do//las kyi rnam par smin pa ma lus pa'i 'jig rten ga du sangs rgyas kyi phrin

21b7(phal chen): pa'i las rnam par dag cing gdung rabs thams cad du ye shes kyi tshul rnam par dag pas/sku tshe'i rgyud kyi tshul dang ldan pa sems can thams cad gdul ba thugs su chud pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yongs
21b8(phal chen): su rdzogs par mdzad do//btsun mo'i 'khor kyi nang du bzhugs pa ston kyang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'dod chags dang bral ba'i tshul gyis gnas la bzhugs pa rnams sgra dang /tshig thams cad dang bral bar
22a1(phal chen): @#//brtags pas/dngos po thams cad dngos po med pa las byung bar khong du chud par bya ba dang/dngos po thams cad shes pa la gzung bar bya ba dang/tshul khrims thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi mthar thug 
22a2(phal chen): par yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang/snying rje chen pos 'gro ba mu med pa la rnam par blta bar bya ba dang /byams pa chen pos sems can med pa'i yul la rab tu rtog par bya ba dang /dga' ba chen pos 'jig rten gyi brjod du

22a7(phal chen): bsgrub pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las phyir mi ldog pa yongs su bstan pa dang/sgo sna tshogs pa nas chos kyi spyod pa rab tu brjod pa dang/sems can rnams kyi bsam pa sna tshogs par rnam par sbyang ba dang /
22a8(phal chen): thugs rje chen po las byung ba'i chos kyi sprin gyis mngon par spang ba dang/thams cad la skyob pa'i byams pa chen po bskyed pa'i cho 'phrul gsum gyis yongs su brlan pa dang/sangs rgyas thams cad mngon pa dang/
22b1(phal chen): rnam par dmigs par btsun mo'i 'khor kyi nang du bstan pa yan chad du 'jig rten mtha' dang dbus med par sangs rgyas kyi phrin las yongs su bstan pa mdzad cing/ye shes kyi mthu' chen po thegs pa med pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi 
22b2(phal chen): mngon par mkhyen pa bsgribs pa med par bsgrubs pa dang /chos rnam pa gsum gyi thabs mkhas pa dang ldan pa'i phrin las kyi mtha' dang/sku'i phrin las kyi mtha' shin tu rnam par dag pa dang/gsung gi phrin las ye shes nyams

22b5(phal chen): rnams 'jig rten gyi gnas las skyod pa dang/'jig rten gyi mngon par zhen pa rnam par bzlog pa dang/'jig rten du gtogs pa thams cad mi rtag par shes par bya ba dang/'jig rten gyi tshul la mngon par zhen pa

23a1(phal chen): @#//kyi zhing bla na med par gyur bar 'gro ba thams cad la bstan pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba'i nang du 'dus par 'gro ba thams cad la yang dag par brjod pa dang /
23a2(phal chen): bdag nyid kyis ye shes kyi don gzigs par bstan pa dang /sems can rnams rnam par dag pa'i sgor brjod pa dang /sems can rnam sa kha na ma tho ba med pa'i sgor bsgrub pa dang /'jig rten thams cad du 'byung ba'i
