23a7(phal chen): cad du gdan rnam par bkram pas yongs su rgyas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing ma lus pa thams cad du byang chub sems dpas rnam par dmigs pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dam pa thob pa rnams kyis zhal du blta ba/byang chub 
23a8(phal chen): sems dpa' thams cad kyi rnam pa dang /smon lam khyad par can dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyis yang dag par bsam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo zab mo rab tu skor ba/sangs rgyas kyi 
23b1(phal chen): yul tshad med pa brtsams pa/byang chub sems dpa' brjod du med pa'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa sna tshogs spyod pa la yang dag par gzhol pa'i byang chub sems dpa' 'khor kyi dkyil 
23b2(phal chen): 'khor rnam par dag pa mnga' ba/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis byin du brlob pa'i yul mnga' ba/dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa'i sems can gyis rnam par dmigs pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba thams 
23b3(phal chen): cad tha mi dad pa dang ldan pa 'byung ba'i sa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa mtha' yas pa'i mthu'i gnas la yon tan gyi khyad par thams cad rab tu brnyes nas bzhugs pa/'jig rten gyi rnam pa thams cad ma lus 
23b4(phal chen): pa'i mthar thug pa/sems can gyi rnam pa thams cad dang /zhig gi rnam pa thams cad dang/chos kyi rnam pa thams cad dang/byang chub sems dpa'i rnam pa thams cad dangyongs su smin par bya ba'i rnam 
23b5(phal chen): pa thams cad dang/dus kyi rnam pa thams cad da/gdul ba'i rnam pa thams cad dang /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba'i rnam pa thams cad ma lusapa'i mthar thug pa'i rnams 'gro ba ma lus pa thams 
23b6(phal chen): cad du mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par 'tshang rgya bas sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin lasalnga pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnam+sa 
23b7(phal chen): kyi chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba mdzad pa'i phyir mi ldog pa'i tshul gyis phyir mi ldog pa'i 'khor lo dang/'jig rten thams cad kyis khong du chud par 'gyur ba'i tshul gyis tshad med pa'i 'khor lo dang/mi 'jigs pa'i 
23b8(phal chen): seng ge'i nga ro chen po'i tshul gyis thams cad ma lus par mngon par sangs rgyas pa'i 'khor lo dang/chos kyi mdzod mu med pa'i sgos snang bar byed pa'i tshul gyis chos thams cad mkhyen pa'i mdzod kyi 'khor lo dang 
24a1(phal chen): @#//ye shes nam mkha'i dbyangs ltar tshad med pa'i tshul gyis chags pa med pa'i 'khor lo dang /chos thams cad kyi dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin rab tu rtogs par bya ba'i tshul gyis bsgribs pa med pa'i 
24a2(phal chen): 'khor lo dang /sems can thams cad kyi chos kyi mig rnam par spyod pa'i tshul gyis 'jig rten thams cad kyi sgron ma'i 'khor lo dang /'jig rten thams cad du rgyas par 'gengs pa'i tshul gyis thams cad mkhyen 
24a3(phal chen): pa'i chos brjod pa'i 'khor lo dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang mi 'gal ba'i tshul gyis sangs rgyas thams cad 'dab 'dres par gyur pa'i 'khor lo dang /de dag la stsogs pa rnam pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya 
24a4(phal chen): stong du/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bas sems can thams cad kyi sems dang spyod pa'i rnam pa la/chos kyi 'khor lo skor te/de ltar chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba sa kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sangs rgyas 
24a5(phal chen): kyi phrin las bsam gyis mi khyab pa mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' sa rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las drug pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni grong dang grong khyer dang grong rdal dang /
24a6(phal chen): rgyal po'i gnas su gshegs pas kyang sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/mi'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /lha'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /klu'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /gnod sbyin gyi rgyal po'i gnas dang 
24a7(phal chen): dri za'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /lha ma yin gyi rgyal po'i gnas dang/nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po'i gnas dang /mi'am ci'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /lto 'phye chen po'i gnas dang /srin po'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /sha za'i 
24a8(phal chen): rgyal po'i gnas dag tu gshegs pas kyang sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi phrin las badzad de/'di lta ste/grong khyer gyi sgor byon nas g.yo ba dang /'od gzer snang ba dang /long ba rnams mig 'thob pa 
24b1(phal chen): dang|'on pa rnams sgra thos par 'gyur ba dang/smos pa rnams dran pa thob par 'gyur ba dang/gos med pa rnams gos thob par 'gyur ba dang /sdug bsngal ba rnams bde ba 'thob pa dang/rol mo'i cha spyad ma dkrol ma brdungs
24b2(phal chen): par sgra yid du 'ong ba rang 'byin pa dang /rgyan snod na 'dug pa dang/gnas nas ma phog pa dang/ma bskyod ma dkrol bar bskyod cing dkrol ba'i sgra 'byung ba dang/bya sna tshogs mang po'i sgra skad yid du 'ong ba 'byung ba dang/blta na
24b3(phal chen): gtang mi bra zhing mi 'thun pa med pa'i sku mthong bas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//mtshan dang dpe' byad bzang po bstan pas kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams
24b4(phal chen): sems can rnams la sangs rgayas kyi phrin las mdzad de/yongs su gzigs pa dang/rnam par gzigs pa dang/brkyang ba dang/bskum pas kyang sems can rnams la sang rgyas kyi phrin las mdzadado//
24b5(phal chen): gshegs pa dang slar bzhud pa dang /bzhengs pa dang/chags pa dang/bzhugs pa dang/gzims pas kyang sems can la sangs rgyas kyi phrin la mdzad do//chos bstan pa dang/mi gsung bar
24b6(phal chen): bzhugs pas kyang sems can rnam la sangs rgyas kyi phrin lam mdzad do//rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul dang/yongs su bstan pa'i cho 'phrul dang/rjes su bstan pa'i cho 'phrul gyis kyang sangs rgyas bcom 
24b7(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams 'jig rten gyi khams kyi yul sems can bsam pa sna tshogs rgya mtsho dang ldan pa 'jig rten gyi spyod pa thams cad rgyu zhing 'byung ba dang ldan pa dge ba'i rtsa ba chen pos shin tu brtan pa/sangs rgyas rjes su
24b8(phal chen): dran pa dang ldan pa/sngon 'grogs 'grogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang gnas pa'i sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni sku gzugs blta
25a1(phal chen): @#//na mi 'oms pa'o zhes bya bar gzhol ba dang/sangs rgyas rnams skye bar bkon no zhes bya bar yid ches par byed pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mthong zhing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung thob pa ni bsod 
25a2(phal chen): nams kyi chos dkar po'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa'o/zhes sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa yid la byed pa dang /'jig rten thams cad rnam par dag pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa skye ba yin no zhes bya bar khong du chud par byed pa dang /
25a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi yon tan bsngags pa ni mtha' yas pa'o zhes bya bar yang dag par lta ba dang /ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs yang dag par bsgrub pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba dang mos pa'i 
25a4(phal chen): tshul thams cad kyis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams mnyes par byed pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ni yon tan mtha' yas pa'o zhes khong du chud par byed pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems can 
25a5(phal chen): thams cad mngon tu gyur par rtog pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems can gyi ra si bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i gnas su gyur par sems pa dang /de bzhin gsheg pa ni zhing thams cad na snang bar khong du chud pa dang 
25a6(phal chen): de lta bu'i dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad 'byung bas 'jig rten thams cad na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//dad par bya ba dang /phyag bya ba dang /
25a7(phal chen): thal mo sbyar bar byas pa dang /rjes su bston pa dang /spyan drangs pa dang /mchod pa dang /mngon du mthong ba dang 'jum pa bya ba dang /sangs rgyas la dga' ba'i sems kyis mos par bya ba'i dge ba'i rtsa 
25a8(phal chen): ba'i sgo nas kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can rnams la sangasargyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//sangs rgyas rjes su dran par gyur pas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams 
25b1(phal chen): ni sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//sangs rgyas mthong bar 'dod par byas pas kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can rnams la sang rgyas kyi phrin las badzad do//
25b2(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku kha dog dang dbyibs kyi rnam pa tshad med pa bstan pas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can rnams sogs la sangs rgyas kyi phrin 'du las mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
25b3(phal chen): gsung thob par bya bas kyang sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams ni semasacan rnams la sangs rgyas gyi phrin las mdzad de/de dag la stsogs pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las
25b4(phal chen): rnam pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa dag/'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /mthu dang 'du la ba ji lta ba'i tshul las mi 'da' bar 'gyur ro//de ltar
25b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi phrin las 'jig rten thams cad kyi sems can kun gyi sems dang spyod pa dang bsam pa la gnas/dge ba rgya che ba tshad med pa dag phyir mi ldog pa'i yul dang /snying rje chen pos rab tu bskul ba dang /
25b6(phal chen): sngon gyi smon lam gyis yongs su skyo ba med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mkhyen pa'i stobs dang /shugs kyis 'jig rten thams cad 'byung ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs
25b7(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las bdun pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni dgon pa la bzhugs sems zhi bar bzhugs sam/dben par bzhugs sam/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa la bzhugs sam/ting nge
25b8(phal chen): 'dzin chen po'i gnas la bzhugs sam/gcig bu dgyes par bzhugs sam/sku mi snang bar bzhugs sam/ye shes zab mo'i gnas la bzhugs sam/sangs rgyas kyi yul gyi stobs tshad med pa la bzhugs sam/
26a1(phal chen): @#//sku'i yongs su spyod pa mi mthong bar bzhugs kyang rung ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gzhan gyis mi tshugs pa'i ye shes dang /smon lam gyi tshul gyis mos pa bzhin 
26a2(phal chen): du 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la rgyun mi 'chad par 'byung ste/sangs rgyas lha yongs su tshol bas kyang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad do//sangs rgyas klu yongs su tshol bas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las
26a3(phal chen): rgyun mi 'chad do//sangs sangs rgyas gnod sbyin yongs su tshol ba dang /sangs rgyas mi 'am ci yongs su tshol ba dang /sangs rgyas lto 'phye chen po yongs su tshol ba dang //sangs rgyas srin po yongs su tshol ba dang/sangs rgyas
26a4(phal chen): mi dang mi ma yin pa yin pa yongs su tshol ba dang / sangs rgyas nyan thos dang /sangs rgyas rang sangs rgyas dang /sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' yongs su tshol bas kyang sa sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyud mi 'chad
26a5(phal chen): do//chos ston pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phril las rgyun mi 'chad do//sangs rgyas gcig brjod pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las rgyun mi 'chad de//sangs rgyas thams cad brjod pas kyang
26a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyan mi 'chad do//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i khyad par dang /smon lam thams cad gcig tu brjod pas kyang sangs rgyasakyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad do//byang chu bsems dpa'i spyod
26a7(phal chen): pa gcig rnam par phye ste/spyod pa tha dad pa brjod pas kyang sangs rgyasagyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chadado//'jig rten gyi yul sangs rgyas kyi yul du brjod pas kyang sangs rgya kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'cha do//sangs
26a8(phal chen): rgyas kyi yul 'jig rten gyi yul du brjod pas kyang  sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gyun mi 'chad do//yul ma yin pa sangs rgyas kyi yul du brdzod pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi srin las rgyun mi 'chad do/nyin mtshan dang
26b1(phal chen): zla ba yar kham mar kham dang /zla ba dang /lo bsgrangs par bzhugs pa dang /yongs su bzhugs pa nas/de bzhin du bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa 'bar du bzhugs pa dang /yongs du bzhugs pas 
26b2(phal chen): kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi phrin las brgyad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 
26b3(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams la 'byung gnas lta bu/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa kun la rnam par spyod pa lta bu/sangs rgyas rnams la dad pa lta bu/sems khong du chud par bya ba la shin tu mos pa lta bu/dbang po 
26b4(phal chen): rnams la bsgrub pa lta bu/sems can skye dgu thams cad la 'dul ba lta bu/'gro ba thams cad la mos pa lta bu/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la 'byung ba lta bu/byang chub tu zhugs pa rnams la skul ba 
26b5(phal chen): lta bu/gzhan gyi drid mi 'jog pa rnams la ye shes kyi snang ba lta bu dag ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni 'jig rten mi rtag par ston pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad par brjod pa dang /phrin las 
26b6(phal chen): mdzad pa'i mthar thug par bstan pa dang /chos kyi rgyun tu nyams pa med pa'i dngos por brjod pa dang /srid pa'i tshul rtsa ba nas chad par brjod pa dang /sems can thams cad skyo bar bya bar bstan pa dang /sangs rgyas 
26b7(phal chen): kyi rjes su 'gro ba'i sems su mthun par bya ba dang /sku tshe'i mtha' lta bur sems can rnams kyis shes par bya ba dang /sems can rnams kyis 'jig rten thams cad du dga' bar bya ba med par bstan pa dang /sems 
26b8(phal chen): can rnams la ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig du brgyud pa mnyes par gyis shig par bstan pa dang /sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnams chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba bstan du mi rung bar khong du chud par bya ba dang /
27a1(phal chen): @#//'jig rten rnams kyi rtsol ba bskyed pa dang sangs rgyas la dmigs par brtson pa'i sems la dgod pa dang /yun tu dran pa rnam par dag pas de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba'i rnam par rtog pa skyed pa dang /skye ba'i 
27a2(phal chen): dbang po phyir bzlog cing yongs su dag par mdzad pa dang /sangs rgyas la mos pa skyed pa dang /skyo bar gyur pa'i sems can rnams la byin gyis brlab par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas la dmigs pa'i rkyen 'jig rten gyi khams ma 
27a3(phal chen): lus pa thams cad du phyin par dgongs pa mdzad pa dang /sku thams cad bshig ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gdung gis sems can rnams yongs su gzung ba mdzad pa dang /'jig rten rnam par 'khrug pa dang /
27a4(phal chen): dregs shing mos pa'i sems can rnams sdom pa la bkod pa mdzad cing /yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bston pas kyang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi 
27a5(phal chen): phrin las dgu pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mya ngan las 'da' ba'i tshe/'jig rten thams cad na sems can rnams smre sngags 'don cing ngu ba dang /mya ngan byed pa dang /'chi ma 'byin pa dang /yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
27a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas zhe'am/thugs rje chen po zhe'am/ded dpon chen po zhe'am/phan par bzhed pa thugs brtse ba can zhe 'am/lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyi sems can thams cad kyi skyabs su gyur pa/mgon du gyur pa/rten 
27a7(phal chen): du gyur pa/bla na med pa/bsod nams kyi zhing /nub par gyur to zhes 'am/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 'byung bar dka'o zhes rnam pa de lta bur smre sngags 'don cing /mya ngan byed pa dang/sangs 
27a8(phal chen): rgyas mthong ba rjes su dran pa'i sems can rnams kyi bsam pa dang /yid la bya ba de dang de dag gi sgor sangs rgyas kyi phrin las tshad med pa rgyun mi chad do/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'a das rnams yongs su mya ngan las 
27b1(phal chen): 'das pa'i tshe/'di lta ste sems can rnams kyi sems dad par bya ba'i dbang dang/sems can rnams dad pa'i mchod par bya ba'i dbang dang /sems can rnams lhag pa'i bsam pa rnam par spyod par bya ba'i dbang dang
27b2(phal chen): sems can rnams yongs su smin par bya ba'i dbang dang/sems can rnams yongasu sbyang bar bya ba'i dbang dang//sems can rnams gdul ba'i dbang dang/sems pa rnams kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs yongs
27b3(phal chen): su rdzogs par bya ba'i dbang dang/sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod pas dmigs par bya ba'i dbang da/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten rnam pa sna tshogs su brgyan pas/phyogs dang yul
27b4(phal chen): sna tshogs su sems can rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba bstsags par bya ba'i dbang gis sems can rnams kyi bsam pa dang mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du sku gdung dang/ring bsrel byin kyis rlob ste/lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin
27b5(phal chen): dang/dri za dang/lha ma ya ni dang/nam mkha' lding dang/mi 'am/ci dang/lto 'phye chen po dang/mi'i gnas thams cad du 'dul ba dang/mos pa'i tshul ji lta ba bzhin du/sku gdung dang ring bsrel byin gyis rlob po//
27b6(phal chen): rang gi sku rnam par phye ste/sku gdung dang/ring srel tshad med pa grangs med pa dpag tu med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa byin gyis rlob//de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchog rten gyis sangs rgyas
27b7(phal chen): kyi sku gdung /'jig rten thams cad du rnam par phye ba de'i yul dang/phyogs kyi klu'i gnas dang/gnod sbyin dang/dri za dang /lha ma yin dang/nam mkha' ldangi dang/mi 'am/ci dag lto 'phye chen po'i gnas dang/
27b8(phal chen): mi'i gnas dang/srin po'i gnas dag tu sku gdung dang/ring bsrel mchod pa byed par 'gyur bas/sku gdung gi ring srel dang dbu dang/phyag sen gyi mchod rten dang/sangs rgyas bzhugs pa'i mchod rten la 'gro ba
28a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad nges par sangs rgyas dran par 'gyur te/chos la rab tu rten par 'gyur ro//dge 'dun la dad pa rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro//yul dang phyogs kyi gnas de dang de dag tu yang rgyun mi 'chad 
28a2(phal chen): par sangs rgyas mchod pa la brtson pa'i bsam pas sbyin pa yongs su gtong bar 'gyur ro//bsod nams byed par 'gyur ro//mtho ris de dang de yongs su dag pa rab tu 'thob par 'gyuro//rigs dang rgyud kyi bdag 
28a3(phal chen): nyid che ba rab tu 'thob par 'gyur to//longs spyod chen po dang/'khor rnam par dag pa rab tu 'thob para'agyur ro//lha dang mi'i phun sum tshogs pa nyams su mnyong bar 'gyur ro//log par ltung ba med pa'i 
28a4(phal chen): lam yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas byung ba don yod pa rab tu sgrub par 'gyur ro//chos dkar po la gnas pa yongs su rdzogs par byed par 'gyur ro//khams gsum pa las 'byung ba'i 
28a5(phal chen): las yongs su spyod par 'gyur ro//theg pa gang la dad pa'i smon lam gyi nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa de dag de dag la drin du gzo ba rab tu 'thob par 'gyu ro//sangs rgyas 
28a6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams la rten pa dang/skyabs bcol ba dang/mgon tsam pa dang/dpung nyen btsam pa'i sems bskyed par 'gyur te/de ltar sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das kyang 
28a7(phal chen): yon tan bsam gyis mi khyab par spyod pa/bsod nams zad mi shes pa'i yon tan gyi gnas dag ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 'jig rten du 'byung na de ltar sems can rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa 
28a8(phal chen): ba rnam pa mang po rab tu bstsags pas bsod nams kyi tshogs rab tu rdzogs par gyur te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bcu po'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag 
28b1(phal chen): ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgya chen po tshad med pa bsam kyis mi khyab pa rnams so//de dag ni sangs rgyas kyi byin rlabs ma gtogs par lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis tshad gzung 
28b2(phal chen): bar mi nus so//'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis 'jug par mi nus pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi 
28b3(phal chen): gnyis su med par spyod cing /dngos po rnam pa gnyis su med pa'i chos kyi dbang phyug gi chos rnams te/bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams ni lung bstan pa thams cad ston pa la gnyis su med par spyod do//
28b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can sangs rgyas kyi rjes su dran pa la brtson pa'i rnams kyi sems dang bsam pa brjod du med pa 'byam klas pa rnams la gnyis su med par spyod//sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad 
28b5(phal chen): kyi don ma lus par mngon par sangs rgyas pas gnyis su med pa'i sku rnam par mdzad do//'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la rang bzhin gnyis su med par thugs su chud pa mnga' 
28b6(phal chen): ba'o//'byung ba dang byang ba'i mtha' gnyis su med pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' dang /da ltar byung ba gnyis su med par thugs sa chud pa mnga' ba'o//sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad gnyis su med pas dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi 
28b7(phal chen): zhing thams cad gcig par rab tu mkhyen cing ston pa thugs su chud pa mnga' ba'o//sang rgyas gcig dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung gnyis su med par thugs se chud pa mnga' ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams
28b8(phal chen): cad kyis thugs su chud pa dang /'dul ba mdzad pa'i rnam par dmigs su med par thugs su chad pa mnga' ba'o//'jig rten gyi chos thams cad dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos gnyis su med par thugs kyi chud pa mnga' ba'o//
29a1(phal chen): @#//'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad dge ba'i rtsa ba gcig ste//gnyis su med par thugs su chud pa mnga' ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag 
29a2(phal chen): ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi gnyis su med par spyod cing //dngos po gnyis su med pa'i chos kyi dbang phyug gi chos so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcung po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'as rnams kyi de bzhin 
29a3(phal chen): gshegs pa bzhugs pa chos thams cad la rab tu zhugs pa rnams so//bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi dbyings gnas pa'i ye shes thugs su chud pa la bzhugs pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
29a4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni thugs rje chen po mi mnyam pa la bzhugs pa'o//sadas rgyas bcom ldan 'das rna ba ni smon lam ji lta ba bzhin du 'byung ba la bzhugs pa'o//sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'dul ba'i 
29a5(phal chen): sems can yongs su mi gtong ba la bzhugs pa'o// sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'angs rnams ni dngos po med pa la gnas pa'i chos nyid la bzhugs pa'o//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasani de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku 
29a6(phal chen): skal pa mnyam pa tha mi dad cing don yod pa la bzhugs pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams ni rtag tu mnyam par bzhag pa'i thugs la bzhugs pa'o//sangs sgras bcom ldan 'das rnams na chos thams cad 
29a7(phal chen): nyams pa med par gzhol ba la bzhugs pa'o//sad rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni chags pa med pa'i dgongs pas rnam par spyod pa la bzhugs pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dang rnams ni chos thams cad kyi 
29a8(phal chen): mnyam pa nyid la 'jug pas grub pa med pa'i yang dag pa'i mtha'i mtshan nyid la bzhugs pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa bzhugs pa chos thams 
29b1(phal chen): cad la bzhugs pa rnams so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas com ldan 'das rnams kyi chos thams cad ma lus par mngon par mkhyen pa rnams te/bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa 
29b2(phal chen): thams cad kyi 'das pa 'grangs pa mtha' dag thams cad mngon par mkhyen pa dang /ma 'ongs pa 'grangs pa mtha' dag/thams cad mngon par mkhyenapa dang /da lta byung ba 'grangs pa mtha' dag tham cad mngon par mkhyen padang /tshig 
29b3(phal chen): gi lam mtha' dag thams cad mngo par mkhyen pa dang /'jig rten gyi rgyud mtha' dag thams cad ma ngon par mkhyen pa dang /sems can gyi bsam pa dang shes pa mtha' dag thams cadamangon par mkhyen pa dang byang chub sems dpa'i dge ba'i 
29b4(phal chen): rtsa ba chu ngu dang 'bring dang chen po dang /rnam pa sna tshogs su rnam par gnas pa'i mtshan nyid kyis dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams la bsam pa tshad med pa yongs su mi gtong bas sangs rgyas kyi ye shes 'bar bar bya ba dang /
29b5(phal chen): yongs su rdzogs par bya ba mtha' dag thams cad mngob par mkhyen pa dang /chos thams cad 'tshang rgya ba'i mkhyen pa mtha' dag thams cad mdon par mkhyen pa dang /'jig rten gyi rgyud thams cad rnam 
29b6(phal chen): par dbye ba'i ye shes gzigs pa mtha' dag thams can mngon par mkhyen pa dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi bzhi mig 'phrul gyi rnam pa lta bur dbye ba thams cad mngon par mkhyen pa ste//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni 
29b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos thams cad ma lus par mngon par mkhyen pa'o////
30a1(phal chen): @#//thug pa med pa/stobs mi nyam pa stobs snying po dang ldan pa/stobs mi shigs pa/stobs 'jig rten thams cad kyis bsam gyis mi khyab pa/stobs sems can thams cad kyis mi 
30a2(phal chen): 'phrog pa/sangs rgyas kyi rgyal mtshan stobs po che/dpa' bo chen po'i stobs rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sangs rgyas kyi sku ni pha rol gyi gnod pa thams cad kyis mi shigs mi chod pa'o//
30a3(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi yul thams cad kyis sku tshe'i dbang po nyams par mi nus pa'o//'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi bskal pa'i me'i gdud pas 'tshang gi par mi nus pa'o//'jigs rten gyi khams thams cad
30a4(phal chen): 'jig par gtogs pa'i chu'i phung por 'grongs par mi 'gyur pa'o//bdud thams cad dang /bdud kyi sde thams cad dang /lha thams cad kyi dbung dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma 
30a5(phal chen): yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /mi dang /mi ma yin pa thams cad kyi dpung dang /sha za dang /srin po thams cad kyi dpung dang/sems can ma lus pa thams cad
30a6(phal chen): kyi dpung gis brgal bar dka' ba'o//rdo rje ri rab gyi ri tsam gyi char ba dang /khor yug gi ri chen po tsam 'am/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i tsam gyi rdo rje'i char bab kyang /bcom ldan 'das de bzhin
30a7(phal chen): gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi ba spu gcig kyang 'gul bar mi 'gyur/g.yo bar mi 'gyur te/bag tsha pa'i shas mi mnga'o//bzhugs sam bzhengs sam
30a8(phal chen): bzhud dam/gzims kyang rung ste/spyod lam 'gyur ba med do//de bzhin gshegs pa gang na bzhugs pa'i yul de dang de'i 'khor dag du yang de bzhin gshegs pa dgyes na kun tu 'bab par mi 'gyur ro//
30b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi zham ring pa rnams la yang gnod par mi 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs pa'i sems can rnams la yang gnod par ma 'gyur zhing /mi g.yon de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku lta smos kyang ci 
30b2(phal chen): dgos te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi chos dang po'o//gzhan yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams dgyes na/chos kyi dbyings 
30b3(phal chen): thams cad kyis klas pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ri rab kyi ri dang bcas pa/khor yug dang khor yug chen po'i ri dang bcas pa/rgya mtsho dang /ri dang nags tshal thibs po dang /sems can gnas pa'i gnas 
30b4(phal chen): tha dad pa kun dang /bcas pa de lta bu'i 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i ba spu'i bu ga gcig tu btsud de/phyi ma'i mtha' bskal pa'i mur thug par bzhag kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin kyi 
30b5(phal chen): rlabs ma gtogs par sems can rnams kyis shes par mi nus so//de bzhin gshegs pa de dag ni de snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams sku'i ba spu'i khung bu gcig gis bzung ste/phyi ma'i mtha' bskal ba'i mur thug 
30b6(phal chen): par bsnams shing /gshegs sam//bzhugs sam/bzhengs sam/gzims kyang rung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs skad cig tsam yang skyo ba mi mnga'o//chud cing dub par mi 'gyur ro//mthu dang 
30b7(phal chen): stobs la yongs su nyam par 'gyur ba mi mnga'o//spyod lam rnam par 'gyur ba mi mang 'o//spyod lam rnam par gyur ba mi mnga'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di lta ste dper na/nam mkha' ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus 
30b8(phal chen): pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 'dzin kyang skyo ba med do//du ba med do//gnod pa med do//med par mi 'gyur ro//mi g.yo mi 'byung mi 'jig go/de bzhin du sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyang pa 
31a1(phal chen): @#//spu'i khung bu gcig du 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad bsnams kyang/de'i rgyus thugs skad cing tsam du yang mi bde ba'i sku nyams mi 'byung ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams 
31a2(phal chen): kyis thu bo che chen po'i stobs/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi chos gnyis po'o//gzhan yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams gshegs stabs gcig tu bzhug pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i 
31a3(phal chen): yang brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi khams yongs su 'da' zhing/dgongs pa bskyed pa gcig gis bskal pa tha dad pa'i rim par sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod dumed pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i rdu la shin tu phra ba snyed 
31a4(phal chen): 'byung ba mdzad do//de lta bur gshegs pas bskal pa 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du gshegs shing/'jig rten gyi khams thams chad kyi sems kyi rgyud ji snyed pa'i bskal par bzhud 
31a5(phal chen): de/'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad kyi rdul shin tu pra ba snyed kyi/'jig rten gyi khams tsam du 'khor kun tu rgya che zhing yangs pa'i khor yug chen po'i rdo rje'i ri dang/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gyi rnam pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du 
31a6(phal chen): med pa la bsres pa snyed kyi rdul shin tu phra bsnyed kyang/de la bsres pa de snyed pa spu'i khung bu gcig tu btsud de/ba spu'i khung bu gcig tu ji lta ba bzhin du/sku'i ba spu'i khung bu thams cad du btsud de/ba spu'i khur bu 
31a7(phal chen): yang sems can thams cad de snyed du gyur la/de dag thams cad bsnams te/de snyed kyi ba spu'i khung bu tshul de bzhin du bshams te/thugs bskyed pa de lta bus/de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi 
31a8(phal chen): khams thams cad du rgyu zhing gshegs sam/phyogs kyi dra ba thams cad du rgyu zhing gshags sam/nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad du rgyu zhing gshigs sam/'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud 'grangs ba sngon gyi mthar 
31b1(phal chen): gtogs pa nas bzung ste/phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur thug par/de bzhin gshegs pas bskal pa thams cad zad par gyur kyang de bzhin gshegs pa bzhud pa la brtson pa ni nyams par mi 'gyur ro//
31b2(phal chen): sku nyams chud par mi 'gyur ro//dgongs pa rnams par 'khrug par mi 'gyur ro//ting nge 'dzin nyams par mi 'gyur ro/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku yongs su nyams par mi 'gyur te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
31b3(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan kyi chos gsum pa'o//gzhan yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bsod snyoms gcig pa zhes nas bskal pa brjod du med 
31b4(phal chen): par skyil mo krung gcig bcas pas/nyin mtshan gyi rgyun tu sdom mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha'i mur gtogs pa'i bskal pa 'das par bzhugs kyang /sku mi g.yo zhing bde ba'i dam pa la spyod pa dang /sangs rgyas 
31b5(phal chen): kyi rnam par spyod pa bsam gyis mi khyab pas rnam par spyod kyang /sems can 'ga' tsam la yang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun 'chad par mi 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku de la 'jig rten gyi khams thams 
31b6(phal chen): cad yongs su bkod de/'jig rten gyi khams de dag gi sems can gcig gi lus kyang 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed tsam du che ba de lta bur 
31b7(phal chen): gyur la/sems can gcig gi lus kyi tshad ji tsam pa de tsam gyi lus su sems can thams cad gyur la/sems can de dag re re'i gzugs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams/thams cad kyi lcid ji tsam yod pa de tsam du gyur 
31b8(phal chen): te/sems can gcig gi lci ba bzhin du sems can thams cad re re'i lcid kyang de tsam du gyur te/sems can de dag thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyag sor kyi rtse mo gcig la/phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i sur 
32a1(phal chen): @#//thug par bsnams te/phyag sor gyi rtse mo gcig gis ji ltar bsnams pa de bzhin du phyag sor thams cad kyi rtse mo la'ang /de tsam re re bsnams kyang /de'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa la sku mnyel pa 
32a2(phal chen): 'am/dgongs pa dub par mi 'gyur te/thugs bskyed pa'i cha shas shin tu phra ba gcig tsam du yang sdug bsngal mi bde ba skye bar mi 'gyur ro//de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams la 'jug pa dang /nam mkha'i dbyings 
32a3(phal chen): la 'jug pa dang /phyogs kyi dra ba'i rnam pa la 'jug pa re re dang de lta bu la 'jug pa nam mkha'i khams ma lus pa'i dbyings kyis klas pa/chos kyi dbyings kyi mtha' gtugs pa skra'i rtse mo'i gzhal pa'i 'jig 
32a4(phal chen): rten gyi khams la 'jug pa thams cad re re zhing yang /skra'i rtse mo'i cha shas kyis gzhal te/nam mkha'i dbyings kyis kun tu khyab pa thams cad ma lus pa'i skra'i rtse mo'i cha shas kyi tshad kyis nam mkha'i dbyings la 
32a5(phal chen): 'jug pa thams cad du zhugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod lam 'di'i tshul dang /skyil mo krung gcig tu bzhugs pa'i spyod pas/sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal pa zad par mdzad de/
32a6(phal chen): bskal pa 'da' par mdzad cing /bskal pa la rab tu 'jug ste//'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi chos bzhi pa'o//gzhan 
32a7(phal chen): yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni sku cig la/dbu sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis byin brlabs te/dbu re re yang ljags sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod 
32a8(phal chen): du med pa'i yang brjod du mad pa'i rdul shin tu phra pa snyed byin gyis brlabs nas/de dag re res kyang dbyangs kyi yan lag dang sgra skad kyi gsung /sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod dumed pa'i yang brjod du medapa'i rdul shin tu phra 
32b1(phal chen): ba snyed re re brjod de /chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa'i sems can gyi khams thams cad go par mdzad cing byangs kyi yan lag dang sgra skad kyi gsung re res kyang mdo sde sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i
32b2(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed bshad la mdo sde re re yang chos bstan pa /sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod dumed pa'i yang brjod du medapa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed rab tu gzhol bar mdzad de /chos bstan pa gcig las kyang chos kyi tshig
32b3(phal chen): dang yi ge sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod tu med pa'i yang rjod du med pa'i rjod rdul shin tu phra ba snyedabarjod par gyur la/chos bstan pa gcig las ji lta ba de bzhin du chos bstan pa thams cad las kyang de snyed kyi chos kyi tshig
32b4(phal chen): dang yi ge brjod de /de ltar brjod pas bskal pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod pa du med pa'i yang brjod tu med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyid du rab tu bstan pa/bskal pa sangs rgyas kyi zhi brjod du med pa'i yang rjod du me dpa'i rdul shin
32b5(phal chen): tu phra ba snyed zad par gyur kyang/da dung gzhan yang bskal pa sangs rgyas kyi zhangi brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu pra ba snyed du brgyud cing rab tu ston pa'i tshul gyis rnam grangs 'dis bskal pa 'jig rten gyi
32b6(phal chen): khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du rab tu bstan la/rnam grangs 'di 'dra bas bskal pa 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdulashin tu phra ba snyed zad la sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi spyod
32b7(phal chen): pa snyed kyi bskal pa 'das par gyur te/phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal pa zad cing 'das par byas kyang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sprul pa chos thams cad snang ba'i 'khor lo bskor ba dang/chos thams cad rab tu shes
32b8(phal chen): par bstan cing/lung bstan pa dang/the tsom thams cad rab tu gcod pa'i 'khor lo bskor ba dang chos thams cad snang bar byed cing /chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba dang/byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa thams cad brjod pa'i rgyun dang ldan
33a1(phal chen): @#//ba'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /ye shes kyi nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i rgyan dang ldan pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor pa dang /'gro ba thams cad ma lus par ye shes kyi sgron ma chen pos brgyan pa'i chos 
33a2(phal chen): kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang/so so yang dag par rig pa dang/mi 'jigs pa chen pos brgyan pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor pa rgyun mi 'chad de/sangs rgyas kyi sku gcig gi byin gyi rlabs kyis ji lta ba bzhin du/chos kyi 'khor 
33a3(phal chen): lo'i sprin chen po dpe thams cad dang/dpe med par rab tu 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de bzhin du chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi nam mkha' dbyings kyi mtha' gtugs pa skra'i rtse mo'i cha shas kyas gzhan pa ma 
33a4(phal chen): lus pa thams cad chags pa med par rnam par dbye bas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa snyed kyi sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa de snyed de snyed du dgongs pa'i skad cig re re la phyi ma'i 
33a5(phal chen): mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal par sprul pa mdzad cing/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sprul pa'i sku thams cad las de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku re re la'ang/de snyed de snyed kyi dbu mnga' bar gyur la gzhung de bzhin du dbu re re la 
33a6(phal chen): yang de snyed de snyed kyi ljags mnga' bar gyur te/ljags re re las kyang de snyed de snyed kyi dbyangs kyi yan lag dang/sgra skad kyi gsung chos kyi dbyings ma lus ba'i sems can gyi khams thams cad go par mdzad pa byung 
33a7(phal chen): la༐ dbyangs kyi yan lag dang /sgra skad kyi gsung de dag re res kyang de snyed de snyed kyimado sde bshad par gyur la mdo sde de dag re re la yang de snyed de snyed kyi/chos bstan pa rab tu gzhol ba mdzad de//chos stan 
33a8(phal chen): pa de dag de re re las kyang /de snyed de snyed kyi chos kyi tshig dang yi ge brjod par gyur la/chos kyi tshig dang yi ge de dag re res kyang chos kyi dbyangs ma lus pa thams cad dbyangs kyis rgyas par bkang ste/sems 
33b1(phal chen): can gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad go bar mdzad kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs la dub pa mi mnga' ste/nyams par mi 'gyur la thogs pa mi mnga' zhing dma' bar mi 'gyur ro//de lta bu'i chos kyi 'khor lo skor 
33b2(phal chen): yang bskal pa phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bar du rgyun 'chad par mi gyur te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan kyi chos lnga pa'o//gzhan 
33b3(phal chen): yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni thugs dpal gyi be'us brgyan pa/rdo rje chen po ltar shin tu brtan par grub pa mnga' ba dag ste/de dag byang chub kyi shing drung du byon na bdud sde gi to can gyis sems can 
33b4(phal chen): thams cad snyed kyi bdud kyi sde/gzugs sna tshogs can shin tu mi sdug pa/'jigs 'jigs ltar 'dug pa/rab tu sngar ba'i gzugs can 'jig rten thams cad shin tu 'jigs pa'i gzugs can rab tu 
33b5(phal chen): skyi g.ya' bskyed pa'i gzugs can/bdud shin tu mi sdug pa'i gzugs 'chad pa rab cog por 'jigs pa/'jigs pa chen po bskyed pa/'gro ba thams cad gsod pa/'jig rten thams cad phung par byed 
33b6(phal chen): pa de lta bu sha stag gis/byang chub kyi shing drung dang /chos kyi dbyings thams cad dang /nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthar thug par gang bar gzigs kyang byang chub kyi shing drung na 'azhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa 
33b7(phal chen): rnams la bsnyengs pa mi mnga'o//bag tsha ba mi mnga'o//ba spu gcig kyang g.yo pa med do//dgongs pa'i cha shin tu phra ba 'ga' tsam gyis kyang de ltar rnam pa tha dad pa'i gzugs la mtshan ma rnam pa tha dad 
33b8(phal chen): 'dzin pa mi mnga' ste/shin tu bstan no//g.yo pa med do//mi sgul to//'gyur ba med do//rnam par rtog pa med do//shin tu zhi'o//snyengs pa dang bag tsa ba thams cad dang shin tu bral lo//sdug pa dang khong khro 
34a1(phal chen): @#//pa shin tu spangs so//sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa rgyun mi 'chad de/byams pa chen po'i stobs dang ldan pa'o//thugs rje chen po'i gnas kyis dbang po'i shin tu dben pa'o//bag chab mi 
34a2(phal chen): mnga' ba'i dgongs pa dang ldan pa'o//dgongs pa shin tu btan zhing nyams pa mi mnga' ba'o//tshus zil kyis mi non la phas zil gis gnon pa'i dgongs pa dang ldan pa ste/bdud thams cad dang /
34a3(phal chen): bdud kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha thams cad dang /bdud kyi sde thams cad shin tu zil kyis gnon to//shin tu spa skod do//de ltar zil gyis mnan te/spa skod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa la 
34a4(phal chen): skyabs tshol lo//de dag la'ang bcom ldan 'das de dag cho 'phrul chen po gsum gyis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdul ba mdzad pa nye bar sgrub ste/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las 
34a5(phal chen): phyir mi ldog par 'gyur ro//'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi chos drug pa'o//gzhan yang sangs rgyas bcom 
34a6(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi gsung thogs pa med pa ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa de dang /de dag thams cad du sems can rnams 'dul ba'i don gyis rnam par dmigs par kun tu khyab par 'byung ste/de 
34a7(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung de ni ri rnams la thogs pa med do//ri rab la thogs pa med do//rin po che'i ri rab la thogs pa med do //khor yug dang khor yug chen po la thog pa med do//lha'i khyim 
34a8(phal chen): la thogs pa med do//klu'i khyim la thogs pa med do//gnod sbyin kyi khyim la thogs pa med do /dri za'i khyim la thogs pa med de//lha ma yin gyi khyim la thogs pa med do//nam mkha' ldig gi khyim la 
34b1(phal chen): thogs pa med do//mi 'am ci'i khyim la thogs pa med do//lto 'phye chen po'i khyim la thogs pa med de//mi dang mi ma yin pa'i khyim la thogs pa med da//gal te brgya la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad na 
34b2(phal chen): yod pa'i sgra chen po 'jig rten rnams tha dad pa'i sgras/'jig rten gyi khams thams chad gang ste/ca co 'dres par gyur kyang /de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa'i gsung ni 'dul ba'i sems can rnams 
34b3(phal chen): kyi rna ba'i rnam par dmigs pa la bsgribs pa med pa yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung 'ba' shig de dag gi rnam par dmigs pa la/lhang nge lham me lhan ne ste go par mdzad pa'i tshig dang /yi ge 
34b4(phal chen): rnams kyang rlag par mi 'gyur te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi chos bdun pa'o//gzhan yang sangs rgyas 
34b5(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chags pa mi mnga' ba ste/bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med par yongs su sbyangs pa'o//'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar 
34b6(phal chen): byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad dang /rgyan gcig tu gyur pa'o//rnyog pa med cing rnam par dang ba ste shin tu gsal ba'o//bdag gi zhes bya ba mi mnga' ba'o//chos thams cad la mi gnas pa'o//
34b7(phal chen): nang na gnas pa ma yin pa'o//phyi rol na gnas pa ma yin pa'o /ma skyes pa'o//dmigs pa thams cad kyis dben pa'o//zhi zhis gnas med pa'o//rtsom pa med pa'o//'du shes sna tshogs thams cad la mi 
34b8(phal chen): gnas pa'o//dngos po'i rnam par rtog pa thams cad 'gags pa'o//rang bzhin gyis 'od dang zhing gsal ba'o//yongs su dag pa'o//dmigs pa dang yid la byas pa thams cad ma 'dres pa'o//chos thams 
35a1(phal chen): @#//cang nyon mongs pa med pa'i yul dang ldan pa'o//yang dag pa'i mtha' 'dod chags dang bral bas dag pa'o//chos kyi dbyings shin tu bstan pa'i mthar thug pa'o//chos kyi dbyings kun nas 
35a2(phal chen): mi zad pa dang ldan pa'o//'du shes kyi rnam pa thams cad kyi tshad dang tshad med pa dang bral ba'o//'dus byas dang /'dus ma byas kyi brjod pa thams cad 'gags pa'o//tshig gi lam gyis brjod du med pa'i 
35a3(phal chen): mthar thug pa'i yul lo//mi zad pa dang /chags pa med pa'i dbyings kyi mthar thug pa'o//ye shes sngon du 'gro ba'o//stobs bcu'i rgyan tu gyur pa'o//chos thams cad kyis rnam par dag pa'o//thabs 
35a4(phal chen): mkhas pa 'byung ba'o//chos sna tshogs dang /chos du ma dang /chos thams cad shin tu bstan pa dang mi 'gal ba'o//dus gsum tha dad pa med pa'o//chos kyi dbyings thams cad du chos gzhi med pa 
35a5(phal chen): thams cad kyi mnga' ba'i pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pa'o//chos thams cad la mnga' ba'i 'byung gnas dang /mdzod du gyur pa'o//chos kyi tshul thams cad la dgongs pa khyad par du rmongs pa med pa'o//
35a6(phal chen): zhing gi rgyud du gtogs pa thams cad dang /phyogs kyi dra ba dang /rgyud 'byam klas pa ma lus pa thams cad la mi gnas pa brtan zhing mi g.yo la nyams pa med pa'i ye shes kyis rab tu gzigs pa/
35a7(phal chen): chos ma lus pa thams cad kyi mthar thug pa/zag pa thams cad yongs su zad pa dgongs pa rnam par grol zhing zag pa mi mnga' ba dang /shes rab rnam par grol bas shin tu rnam par grol ba yang 
35a8(phal chen): dag pa'i mtha' mi zad pa'i klas pa/chags pa med par rnam par spyod pa/rtag tu mnyam par gzhag pa/dus gsum thams cad du bsgribs pa med par 'byung ba/sems can thams cad kyi dus 
35b1(phal chen): gsum gyi sems dang /spyod pa thams cad ma lus par thugs skad cig gis rab tu mkhyen pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan 
35b2(phal chen): gyi chos brgyad pa'o//gzhan yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi chos kyi sku ni sku mi mnga' ba ste/rang bzhin gcig tu gyur la/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yul tshad med pa'i sku'o//mtha' 
35b3(phal chen): dad dpung med par yongs su grub pa'i sku'o//'jig rten thams cad kyis zad par ma nus pa'i sku'o//khams gsum pas gos pa med pa'i sku'o//rten pa'i skus rnam par dmigs pa'o//lhag 
35b4(phal chen): pa'i bsam pa dang /rjes su dran pa'i byin kyis rlabs kyis sku'i rang bzhin yang dag par stan pa'o//bden pa med pa dang /brdzun pa med pa'i sku mnyam pa nyid kyis yongs su dag pa'o//chos kyi sku 
35b5(phal chen): bzhud pa dang /byon pa med pa'i sku'o//'dus ma byas pas 'jigs pa med pa'i sku'o//mtshan nyid med pas mtshan nyid gcig pa'i chos kyi sku'i rang bzhin no//yul na mi gnas pa dang /phyogs 
35b6(phal chen): na mi gnas pa'i sku'o//ma lus pa thams cad kyi lus kyi sku'o//sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog gi mthu byung ba dang /rnam par 'phrul pa mtha' yas pa sku'i rang bzhin no//lus thams cad du yongs su 
35b7(phal chen): bzhol ba'i sku sna tshogs su rnam par dmigs pa mdzad kyang chos kyi sku'i tshul gyi rjes su 'brang ba'o//ye shes kun tu snang ba'i snying po can no//sku yongs kyi mdzod du gyur pa'o//chos kyi dbyings rnam pa 
35b8(phal chen): tha dad pa med pa shin tu bstan pa'i sku'o//khams ma lus pa thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pas/kun tu khyab pa'i sku'i rang bzhin no//mi g.yo mi dgongs pa'i sku rnam par dag pa'o//yod pa ma yin med pa'ang 
36a1(phal chen): @#//ma yin pa'i tshul dang tshul ma yin par rab tu brjod pa'i sku'o//sems can gyi mos pa rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul bar 'gyur ba'i sku'o//'gags pa'ang ma yin ma 'gags pa'ang ma yin pas 
36a2(phal chen): 'dul ba ston pa'i sku'o//yon tan rin po che thams cad yang dag par ston pa'i sku'o//yon tanar rin po che thams cad yangadag par byung bas shin tu 'phags pa'i sku'o//chos thams cad sangs rgyas kyi chos 
36a3(phal chen): kyi de bzhin nyid du yang dag par bsgrubs pa'i sku'o//rang bzhin gyis rab tu zhi zhing pa sgribs pa med pa'i mthar thug pa'i sku'o//thogs pa mi mnga' bas chos thams cad thugs su chud pa'i sku'o//thams cad kyi 
36a4(phal chen): rjes su song ba'i chos kyi dbyings kyis rnam par dag cing /'jig rten gyi thams cad shin tu rnam par phye ba'i sku'o//phyir mi ldog cing bsgribs pa mi mnga' la/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pas ye shes thams 
36a5(phal chen): cad kyi rjes su 'brang zhing /yang dag par gzhol bas/thugs su chud pa'i sku te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mthu bo che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan kyi chos dgu pa'o//
36a6(phal chen): gzhan yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi ye shes mnyam pa med pa dang /mnyam pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis thugs su chud pa ste/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad 
36a7(phal chen): kyis byung ba'o//mngon par bsgrub pa'i spyod pa khyad par du 'phags pa'i smon lam dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pas rnam par dag pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod ba thams cad kyis yongs su 
36a8(phal chen): rdzogs par byas so//tha dad pa med pa'o//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyodapa thams cad kyis byung ba'o//byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa rgya mtsho'i spyod pa'i tshul thams cad thugs su chud 
36b1(phal chen): pa'o||smon lam dang spyod pa chen pos yongs su mi 'dogs pa'o//spyod pa thams cad mi mnyam pa med pas yongs su rdzogs par byas pa'o//spyod pa'i stobs la thag par dka' ba'i spyod pa thams cad kyis 
36b2(phal chen): mngon par byung ba'o//spyod pa thams cad kyis rnam par dag pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa legs par spyad pas rgyun dma' ba med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad kyis tshad med par 
36b3(phal chen): spyod pa'o//ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyis spyod yul mtha' yas pa'o//lam thams cad kyis rab tu brjod pa ma yin pa'o//lam gyi yul med pa'o//lam thams cad las rab tu zhugs pa'o//ye shes dam pa'i stobs 
36b4(phal chen): kyis tshul mkhyen pa'o//bag tsha ba mi mnga' bas/chos kyi char 'bebs pa'i 'khor lo dang ldan pa'o//dge ba dris pa dang /ma dris pa la rjes su 'thun pa'i chos kyi tshul ston pa mkhyen pa'o//mnyam pa med pa dang /mnyam pas 
36b5(phal chen): shin tu dag pa'o//sku dang thugs dang gsung gi yang dag par spyod pa rnams la phrin las gsum yongs su dag pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi bsgom pas brnyes so//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs thams cad bskyed pa'i 
36b6(phal chen): don du sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyis mdzad pa thams cad rab tu 'byung ba'o//thams cad mkhyen pa'i gnas kyi bstan pa tshad med pa'i gnas rtsa ba med pa dang ldan pa'o//mngon par shes pa'i ye shes thams 
36b7(phal chen): cad kyis mthar thug par mi 'gyur ba'o//'jig rten gyi sems kyi spyod pa'i shes pa thams cad kyis bsam gyis mi khyab po//chos thams cad ma lus par mkhyen cing gzigs pas sems can thams cad 
36b8(phal chen): yongs su skyob pa'o//dus gsum yangs pa thams cad kyi tshul shin tu phra ba mtha' dang dbus med pa ma lus par mkhyen pa'o//'jig rten las 'das pa'i don mkhyen pa'i phyir phra ba'i ye shes thugs su chud pas 
37a1(phal chen): @#//pas 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad rnam par dag pa'i phyir mi ldog pa'i ye shes kyis sangs rgyas kyi phrin las brjod du med pa 'jig rten thams cad du sbyor ba'o//sangs rgyas kyi sku 
37a2(phal chen): grangs pa thams cad thugs su chud pa'i ye shes kyis yi ge med pa'i chos thugs su chud pa'o//ye ge sna tshogs rnam par phye pa dang /yi ge thams cad smos shing brjod pa mkhyen pas/brjod du med pa 
37a3(phal chen): thugs su chud pa'o//kun tu bzang po'i ye shes kyis dge ba'i rtsa ba'i chos spyad pa thams cad rnam par dang pa'o//dam bcas pa'i tshul bzhin du sems can thams cad kyi lus dang skye mched ji ltar dgongs 
37a4(phal chen): pa'i skad cig dang ldan pa'i shes rab kyis chos thams cad khong du chud par ston pa mkhyen pa'o//chos thams cad kyi don du chos yongs su btang zhing rim gror bya ba sa thugs su chud pa'o//'jig rten gyi khams 
37a5(phal chen): thams cad ma lus par yul du gyur pa'o//chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad yul du gyur pa'o//dus gsum thams cad la dgongs pa'i skad cig gis chags pa med par mkhyen cing thugs su chud pa'i yul 
37a6(phal chen): dang ldan pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sangs rgyas 'byung ba ma lus par yul du gyur ba'o//'jigs pa med par 'dul ba thams cad yul du gyur ba'o//thams cad kyi rjes su song zhing rnam par dag 
37a7(phal chen): pa'i yul thams cad mngon par sangs rgyas pa'o//sa mtshams med cing thug pa med pa'i mngon par shes pa rnam par sangs rgyas pa'o//sems can thams cad kyi sems dang yid dang rnam par shes pa'i rnam 
37a8(phal chen): pa dus gsum du brjod pa ma lus pa thams cad dgongs pa'i skad cig gi mkhyen cing thugs su chud ba'o//sems can gyi rnam pa thams cad las mtshams ma bcad pa'o//'jig rten gyi khams kyi rnam pa thams cad la 
37b1(phal chen): mu med pa'o//dus gsum gyi rnam pa thams cad la mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog gi mthu byung ba'i rnam pa thams cad la tshad med pa'o//shes bya'i rnam pa thams cad la lha ma ma lus pa ste/de dag 
37b2(phal chen): thams cad lhag ma med par mngon par sangs rgyas pas sangs rgyas kyi ye shes sangs rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul dang rnam par 'phrul bas byung ba thams cad thugs su chud pa dang /mkhyen pa thams cad rgyun 'chad pa med de 
37b3(phal chen): de la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog gis mthu byung ba'i skus/mtha' dang dbus med par spyod pa yin/chags pa med pa'i dgongs pas spyod pas mtha' dang dbus med par spyad pa yin 
37b4(phal chen): thugs rjes chen po'i sbyor bas dus thams cad du rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par chos bstan pa la spyod pas mtha' dang dbu med par spyod pa yin te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthu 
37b5(phal chen): po che chen po'i stobs sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa'i rgyal mtshan kyi chos bcu pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi mthu po che chen po'i stobs sangasargyas kyi spyod pa'i 
37b6(phal chen): rgyal mtshan gyi chos rnams te/'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sems can thams cad dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis bsam gyis mi khyab cing dpag tu mi nus pa dag 
37b7(phal chen): go/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu pa 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi thogs pa mi mnga' ba rnams te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni dga' ldan gyi tshe/ji srid par bzhugs nas 'pho ba la 
37b8(phal chen): thogs pa mi mnga'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni zla ba bcur bltam pa bstan pa la thogs pa mi mnga'o//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni mngon par 'byung ba'i dgyes pa'i dgongs pas 
38a1(phal chen): @#//mngon par 'byung ba la thogs pa mi mnga'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni byang chub kyi shing drung du bzhugs nas sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad mngon par 'tshang rgya ba la thogs pa mi mang 'o//
38a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams dgongs pa'i skad cig gis sangs rgyas kyi chos thamasacad mngon par sangs rgyas nas 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa 
38a3(phal chen): thams cad yongs su bstan pa la thogs pa mi mnga'o/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni dus bzhin du 'dul ba rnams la chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba la thogs pa mi mnga'o //sangs rgyas bcom ldan
38a4(phal chen): rnams ni dge ba'i rtsa ba ji ltar bskyed pa'i tshul bzhin du dus la bab par bshad cing lung bstan pa la thogs pa ming mnga'o//sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams ni dus la bab pa'i tshul bzhin du sangs rgyas
38a5(phal chen): kyi phrin las mdzad pa la thogs pa mi mnga'a'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni byang chub sems dpa' yongs su grub pa rnams lung bstan pa la thogs par mi mnga'o//sangs rgyas bco ma ldan
38a6(phal chen): 'das rnams ni skad cig gcig tu sems can thams cad kyis dri ba yongs su dris pa skad cig gcig gis lung bstan pa la thogasapa mi mnga' ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan
38a7(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi thogs pa mi mnga' ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'dasarnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ma thag tu myur du 'byung ba'i chos rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/
38a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mthong ma thag tu sems can rnams myur du ngan song dang bral bar 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni mthong ma thab tu sems can rnams myud du bsod
38b1(phal chen): nams yongs su rdzogs pa 'thob par 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mthong ma thag tu sems can rnams myur du dge ba'i rtsa ba la yongs su smin par 'gyur ro//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das
38b2(phal chen): rnams ni mthong ma thag tu soms can rnams myur du mtho rasi su skye ba yongs su dag pa 'thob par 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams ni mthong ma thag du sems can rnams myur tu yid gnyis dang/nem nur yang dag
38b3(phal chen): par bstsal ba 'thob par 'gyur ro//langs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni mthong ma thag tu sams can rnams myur dubyang chub kyi sems bskyed nas phyir mi ldog pa 'thob par gyur te/sems can mos pa yangs pa dang ldan pa

38b6(phal chen): par dag par 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'i 'das rnams ni mthong mthug tu sems can rnams myur dubasgribs pa thams cad byang ba thob par 'gyur ro//sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnamasani mthong ma thag tu
38b7(phal chen): sems can rnams myur dusnang ba 'thob cing mi 'jigs pa zhum pa med pa'i spobs pa rab tu rtsom ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ma thag tu myur du
38b8(phal chen): 'byung ba'i chos rnams so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi rjes su dran pa rnam par dag pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni sngon gyi sbyor bas kyang
39a1(phal chen): @#//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rjes su dran par bya ba yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni spyod pa rnam par dag pas kyang byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyis rjes su dran par bya ba yan/sangs rgyas 
39a2(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs pas kyang byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyis rjes su dran par bya pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom pa ldan 'das rnams ni smon lam chen po yongs su rdzogs pas kyang

39a4(phal chen): rnams ni sngon gyi mthar sangs rgyas kyi tshangs par spyod pa la spyad pas kyang byang chub sems ngapa' rnams kyis rjes su drang par bya ba yin/sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams mngon par 'tshang rgya ba'i sko nas kyang byang

39a7(phal chen): ma'i rigs pas kyang byang chub semasad pa'i rnams kyis rjes su dran par bya ba yan te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi rjes su dran pa rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i

72b2(phal chen): rnams bris/de dag thams cad rab tu shes//sangs rgyas ye shes tshul la rab tu 'jug/'khor rnams brjod du med pa dag/sangs rgyas rnams re re dag la yod pa'i rnams//kun la 'jug cing mthong
72b3(phal chen): bar byed/sems can rnam par 'dul ba'i mtha' 'ang med//ye shes zab mo mthong bas na//ye shes nyi ma shin tu gsar bar shar gyur pa'i//phyir mi ldog pa'i spyod pa la//'jig rten 'byam klas kun tu 
72b4(phal chen): 'jug par byed//ji lta'i tshul du zhugs gyur cing //'jig rten 'di dag thams cad rnam par bkod 'dra bar//sems can tshul bzhin rab tu 'phags//khams gsum thams cad mig yor tshul 'drar 
72b5(phal chen): shes||'on kyang rtog dang rnam rtog med//'jig rten mtha' dang dbus med kun la rtog mi byed//rtog dang rtog med mkhas pa ste//rtog pa'i shes pas rnam par rtog pa med//bskal pa brjod du med pa'i rnams 
72b6(phal chen): sems bskyed gcig la yang dag 'du bar 'dzud byed cing //'jig rten mi khom 'di mthong nas//skad cig sems kyis thams cad shes par byed//brjod du med pa'i zhing rnams kyang //sems bskyed gcig
72b7(phal chen): gis thams cad yongs su 'da' bar byed//bskal pa brjod med zad byed byed//skra rtse'i cha shas gcig las g.yo ba med//bskal pa brjod dumed pa dag/nyi ma gcig dang yud tsam gcig tu 'dzud byed 
72b8(phal chen): kyang||ring dang thung la gzhol ba med//skad cig gcig la 'jug pa'i pha rol phyin//sems kyi rnam pa 'jig rten kun//'jig rten rnam pa sems can kun gyi sems yin no//rnam pa gnyis kyi grangs su 
73a1(phal chen): @#//zhugs/'on kyang de dag mi gnyis rtog pa med//sgyu ma'i rnam pa 'jig rten te//sems can zhing dang sangs rgyas chos kyang de bzhin no//'on kyang 'jig rten mgon po'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings 
73a2(phal chen): rnams kun la gcig tu snyoms//lus rnams brjod du med pa yis//phyogs bcu thams cad rgyas par bkang zhing yongs su ston//'on kyang lus la gzhol ba med//chos nyid rten pa gnas med rab tu shes//
73a3(phal chen): 'on kyang mi'i seng ge rnams//gnyis la gnas pa'i shes pas byung ba ma yin te//mi gnyis pa la 'ang gnas pa med//gnyis dang gnyis med chos rnams rab tu shes//ji ltar smig rgyu rab rib dang //
73a4(phal chen): mig yor sgra bsnyan de bzhin rmi lam 'dra ba dang //rmi lam rnam pa sprul pa ltar//'gro ba thams cad ma lus rab tu shes//de ltar spyod yul nyams med pas//sangs rgyas kun kyi rigs kyi rjes su 'byung 
73a5(phal chen): ba yin//kun tu bzang po'i ye shes dang //chos kyi dbyings la shin tu bdal ba yin//snying rje chen po mtha' yas kyang //sems can med dang mnyam par zhing rnams thams cad sgom//sngon nas nyon mongs nyams 
73a6(phal chen): med pa'i//bsam pa dag pas 'jig rten kun tu 'gro//mi zad mkha' ltar rnam par dag pa'i//rgya che shin tu yangs pa'i sa la rab tu zhugs//gsung ba'i seng ge thams cad kyi//tshul gyi dkyil 'khor thams 
73a7(phal chen): cad rjes su dran//'jig rten rab tu spros pa la//smon lam tshad med yongs su gdab pa byas pas na//de dag spyod pa yongs su dag/'jig rten khams rnams thams cad rgyas par khyab ba//sangs 
73a8(phal chen): rgyas rnams dang rgyas sras dang //de bzhin sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos rnams thams cad dang //'jig rten rnams dang 'jig rten chos//chags med tha dad med pa'i tshul du shes//chos kyi sku'i snying 
73b1(phal chen): po can||de'i lus la 'jig rten thams cad gzung byas shing //'jig rten de'i lus yin yang //'jig rten kun kyis de la gos pa med//ji ltar dang ba'i chu gsal la//gzugs brnyan yongs su snang yang 
73b2(phal chen): der song 'phos ba med//de bzhin 'jig rten thams cad kyi//rjes su lus 'gro dang song 'phos ba med//ji ltar 'jig rten thams cad kyis//gos pa med cing 'jig rten yongs su dag pas na//lus ni 
73b3(phal chen): mkha' dang 'dra ba ste//de'i lus rnams gang la yang skyes pa med//mi zad pa'i lus shes pas//de ni nam yang skyes shing 'gags pa med pa ste//rtag dang mi rtag ma yin par//'jig rten kun la 'am rnam 
73b4(phal chen): par dmigs pa byed//lta ba'i rnam pa rab tu 'grogs//brten pa de dag yang dag blta ba ston pa byed//bdag gi med cing gnas pa med//len pa med cing chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam//ji ltar sgyu mkhan mkhas 
73b5(phal chen): pa zhing||sna tshogs mtshan nyid rnam pa tha dad gshegs 'phrul byed//'on kyang de la 'gro ba med//gang du'ang 'gro ba de bzhin mi dmigs so//sgyu ma tshad med ma yin te//sgyu ma tshad kyis dmigs par bya ba'ang ma 
73b6(phal chen): yin no//tshad med tshad dang ldan par yang //tshogs kyi dbus su ston par rab tu byed//de bzhin rtogs pa'i blo ldan pa//sangs rgyas kun kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams las byung //sems ni tshad yod ma yin te//tshad med 
73b7(phal chen): pa la gnas pa'ang de dag min//'du shes rnam pa gcig min te//de dag tshad med pa dang tshad dang ldan pa ston//lam gyi rgyud dang 'brel ba med//tshad med rnams la gnas pa'ang de bzhin med//sangs rgyas chos 
73b8(phal chen): rnams zab pa ste//shin tu yangs pas zab cing zhi bas de bzhin zab//zab mo sna tshogs rgyud rnams dang //mtha' yas zab pa rnams kyang rab tu shes//ye shes bzang po rmongs pa med//rgyud la dri me med pas 
74a1(phal chen): @#//bsam pa rnam par dag/byin kyis rlob la mkhas pas na//srog chags bye ba shin tu dga' bar byed//dge bar byed pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams//rab tu brjod cing dge dang mi dge rnam par ston//gnas 
74a2(phal chen): la mngon par ma chags pas//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun tu rab tu rgyu//sems can med mthar phyin gyur kyang //'jig rten thams cad ma lus par//rnam par sangs rgyas mya ngan gar mi 'da'//chos rnams 
74a3(phal chen): bgrang ba'i tshul la mkhas//sems can bgrang tshul shes kyang grangs la skyes pa med//chos kyi sprin chen char 'bebs shing //'jig rten khams rnams thams cad snang bar byed//sangs rgyas byang chub ma lus 
74a4(phal chen): la||sems bskyed gcig gis de dag rab tu 'jug/de dag phyir ldog med pa ste//sems can kun kyi khams las phyir mi ldog/sangs rgyas sku ni thob gyur kyang //chos rnams 'di dag lus yon ma 
74a5(phal chen): yin rab tu shes//lus rnams kun kyi tshul la mkhas//lus kyis rnam phye 'jig rten rab tu shes//sems can kun tu rgyu ba dang //bskal pa rnams dang zhing la pha rol phyin pa thob//phyogs rnams rgya 
74a6(phal chen): mtshor ye shes rgya mtsho'i tshul la mkhas pas zhugs//lus rnams thams cad re re las//sems can kun gyi lus rnams sna tshogs ji snyed dang //sangs rgyas kun ni mtha' yas pa//ye shes shin 
74a7(phal chen): tu yangs pa de ston to//skad cig gcig gi dus tshul la//mi'i gtso bo mang po 'byung ba rab tu shes//bskal pa mang po brjod med du//mi'i seng ge bgrang bas mi zad 'gyur//de dag mya ngan 'da' ba yang //
74a8(phal chen): skad cig gcig la tshad med shin tu yangs pa 'byung //ring bsrel rnam dbye mang po yang //ye shes dri med phra bas ston pa byed//de bzhin ma 'ongs dus kyi yang //mi'i seng ge mtha' yas smon lam 'debs pa'i 
74b1(phal chen): rnams/byang chub dri med sems bskyed pa//ye shes yangs pa shin tu phra bas shes//bzang po'i spyod la gnas bcas shing//de bzhin dus gsum gshegs pa sems can gtso bo rnams//de snyed skyes par 'gyur 
74b2(phal chen): ba dag/de dag thams cad ma lus rab tu shes//de ltar rab tu shes pas na//mtha' dang dbus med spyod pa'i sa dang de bzhin du/ye shes 'byung gnas blo gros la//ye shes 'khor lo'i spyod pas phyir mi ldog/ye shes 
74b3(phal chen): phra mo rab yangs pa//stobs bcu rnams kyi spyod yul mchog la de dag zhugs//bzang po rtogs pa'i blo can rnams//phyir mi ldog la 'jug pa brjod byas na//sangs rgyas rgyal ba thams cad kyi//yang dag gzhol 
74b4(phal chen): bar bya la de dag zhugs pa na//spyod las phyir ldog med pa ste//byang chub dam pa zhi bar 'tshang rgya yang//de 'dra sems rgyud gcig la yang //mtha' dang dbus med sems rnams yang dag 'dzud pa byed//shin tu yangs 
74b5(phal chen): pa'i tshad med las//'du shes de dang de la yang dag bsgrubs//nyon mongs med dang nyon mongs dang //slob dang mi slob sems kyang de bzhin rab tu shes//sems rgyud de dag re re la'ang //sems rnams brjod du 
74b6(phal chen): med pa mngon 'tshang rgya//gcig dang gnyis su shes pa med//yang dag yongs su 'dres par 'gyur ba 'ang med pa ste//so so'i 'du shes rnam pa la//sems can de dag skyes par gyur pa yin//'di na 'dug par 
74b7(phal chen): mthob gyur kyang//sems can brjod med gzhan dang gzhan dang gzhan sems ldan pa'i rnams//gzhan gyi zhing phyir 'du shes kyang //'jig rten sna tshogs rnams la rab tu skye//'di 'dra'i tshul la 'dug pa la//spyod 
74b8(phal chen): pa'i mchog gis rjes su zhugs par gyur pa'i rnams//kun tu bzang pa'i ming thob ste//'jig rten mgon po kun gyi rjes su skyes//sems can bye ba la la ni//ngan song 'grangs pa'i ming thogs mtho ris 'du shes 
75a1(phal chen): @//'gyur la la mtho ris sa rnams kyi//rnam pa kun la ngan song 'du shes 'gyur//'jig rten mi mtshungs skal la ltos//rnam pa tha dad las kyi sa las rab tu skyes//'du shes rnam pa mtha' yas te//gcig 
75a2(phal chen): min yangs la 'jig rten rab tu 'jug/'jig rten khams rnams thams cad kyi//'du shes dra ba skal pa mi mnyam rab tu shes//mig 'phrul sgyu ma'i tshul gyis na//skad cig gcig gis sems kyis rab tu gzhol//
75a3(phal chen): de bzhin dbang po rnams la ste//mig gi skye mched rna bsna'i skye mched dang //lce dang lus dang yid la yang //'jig rten rnam pa tha dad 'du shes 'jug/mig rnams re re'i yul la yang //mtha' dang dbus med mig 
75a4(phal chen): rnams rab tu rdzud pa byed//rnam pa tha dad du ma ste//tshad med kun la'ang zhen par 'gyur ba med//'on kyang phan tshun gcig gis gcig/bde zhing gnod par 'gyur ba de dag kun na med//bdag bdag so so'i las kyi rnams//
75a5(phal chen): ji ltar bstsags pa'i tshul bzhin nyams su myong //bzang zhes bya ba de dag kun//tshad med stobs dang //ye shes sa'i shes rab kyis//'byam klas mig rnams thams cad la//rab tu zhugs pas shin tu rtogs pa 
75a6(phal chen): yin//de bzhin 'jig rten thams cad kyang //thams cad ma lus rnam pa thams cad 'jig tshul du//thams cad shin tu shes gyur kyang //rgya chen spyod pas mya ngan mi 'da'o//sangs rgyas shin tu bstan pa dang //sems can 
75a7(phal chen): bstan dang de bzhin zhing rnams bstan pa dang //dus rnams shin tu bstan pa dang /bstan pa sna tshogs rnam pa kun kyang shes//la la 'di la 'das gyur kyang //gzhan dang gzhan rnams ma 'ongs dus la gnas pa yin//'on kyang 
75a8(phal chen): ma 'ongs dus kyi rnams//la las da ltar byung bar rab tu shes//de 'dra'i tshul can gcig min pa'i//'jig rten mtshan nyid tha dad thams cad rab tu shes//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid tshul gyis//sangs rgyas ye shes 
75b1(phal chen): kun tu mtha' 'ang yas//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas pha la po che zhes bya ba las/kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa bstan pa'i le'u ste/bzhi bcu gnyis pa'o////sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya 
75b2(phal chen): ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mod////bam po brgyad pa////bcom ldan 'das ye shes chen pos rnam par sangs rgyas pa'i dgongs pa mnga' ba 'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad 
75b3(phal chen): dang //sku gcig pa'i mnyam pa nyid brnyes pa//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi spyod pa mnyam pa nyid dang ldan pa/chags pa med pa'i spyod yul mngon par mkhyen pa/ye shes kyi rang bzhin gyi chos kyi sku mnga' ba/mtshan nyid yod 
75b4(phal chen): nas med pa'i chos la rten pa/chos kyi dbyings mi gnyis pa'i spyod yul gyis yang dag par spyod pa/mtha' dang dbus med cing mtshungs pa'i mngon par 'tshang rgya ba brnyes pa/rnam par thar pa bsgribs pa med pa'i spyod 
75b5(phal chen): yul la shin tu dgyes pa/chos kyi sku nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas klas pa mnga' ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin kyi rlabs kyi pho brang khang pa brtsegs pa'i gzhal med khang de bzhin gshegs pa bzhugs pa/chos kyi 
75b6(phal chen): dbyings kyi snying po can/'od gzer kun nas 'byung ba rgyan chen po dang ldan la/'od dang zhing gsal ba/rnam par snang ba'i snying po can sangs rgyas skye ba chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa'i gnas/byang chub sems dpa'i 
75b7(phal chen): lus las byung ba gzhi/'od gzer gyis rnam par brgyan pa/byang chub sems dpa'i lus rang bzhin du gnas pas rab tu bsgrubs pa/chos chen po'i gzhir gyur pa/chos kyi dbyings kun tu rgyas pa/de bzhin gshegs 
75b8(phal chen): pa'i 'od dang ldan pa/nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mtha' gtugs pa/ye shes kyi yul yang dag pa'i mtha' spyod yul chags pa med pas rnam par gsal ba na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rin po che bzang po'i 
76a1(phal chen): @#//pad mo chen po rang rang gi smon lam gyis byung ba'i dbus kyi snying po la 'khor ba'i/kun kyang tshe gcig gis thogs pa/'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dang gzhan nas phyin pa/ye shes chen po rnam par phye ba'i 
76a2(phal chen): spyod yul la gzhol ba/chos kyi dbyings tha mi dad pa'i gzhi la mnyam pa nyid la zhugs pa/nam mkha'i dbyings bzhi med cing /mtha' dang dbus med pa la rnam par spyod pa/chos thams cad 'byung ba med cing /mngon 
76a3(phal chen): par grub pa med pa mngon du byas pa/byang chub sems dpa'i lus las g.yas g.yos su thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rab tu thob pa/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du kun kyis brjod pa/de bzhin gshegs 
76a4(phal chen): pa'i ye shes pas mngon du byung ba/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byang chub mngon sum du gyur pa'i ye shes snang ba thob pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul rgyun mi chad pa/ye shes kyi rang bzhin chos kyi lus gcig 
76a5(phal chen): cing tha mi dad pas/gzugs kyi lus mtha' yas pa'i sgo kun ston pa/chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa 'jug pa shin tu shes pa/sems can kyi khams su rnam par spyod pa las phyir mi ldog pa/de bzhin 
76a6(phal chen): gshegs pa'i chos kyi dbyings dang /yang dag pa'i mtha' mnyam pa nyid thob pas sprul pa mngon par sgrub pa/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad dang /sngon lhan cig du skyes shing dge ba'i rtsa ba bstsags pa 
76a7(phal chen): sha stag 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po dang /kun tu grags pa'i dbang phyug rgyal po dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su byung ba'i dpal dang /rdo rje dpal gyi rgyal mtshan dang /
76a8(phal chen): rnam par grol ba bsgribs pa med pa'i zla ba dang /nyi ma'i 'od zer dri ma med pa'i snying po dang /mngon par shes pa chen pos rnam par 'phrul pa'i blo dang /'od dri ma med pa'i dpal gyi gzi brjid 'od zer gyi rgyal po dang /
76b1(phal chen): blo gros rgya mtsho'i mchog 'dzin cing /blo la rnam par rtse ba/gsal bas mngon par shes pa'i dpal dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po stobs bcus mi 'jigs pa'i seng ge'i nga ro chen pos rnam par 
76b2(phal chen): rtse bas mngon par shes pa dang /de dag las stsogs pa byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa phrag bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//
76b3(phal chen): de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi mdzod spu nas de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer 'od zer gyi 'khor bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa mang po'i 'od zer dang ldan pa byung ste//des 'jig rten 
76b4(phal chen): gyi khams 'byam klas pa thams cad du ma lus par snang byas//'jig rten g.yas phyogs su lan bcur bskor te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa bstan nas/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba khrag khrig brgya 
76b5(phal chen): stong mang po bskul ba byas te/'jig rten gyi khams 'byam klas pa tham sa cad rab tu bstod nas//ngan song gi rgyud thams cad kyi skye ba dang /shi 'pho ba rab tu zhi bar byas te/bdud kyi khyam thams cad zil gyis mnan 
76b6(phal chen): nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mngon par 'tshang rgyas so//rnam par sangs rgyas pa'i gdan bstan pa byas te//sangs rgyas kyi 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par bkod pa//'byam klas pa rab tu bstan nas/'jig rten 
76b7(phal chen): gyi khams chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/nam mkha'i mthas gtugs par 'byam klas pa thams cad ma lus par rgyas par khyab par byas nas/phyir 'ongs te/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad 
76b8(phal chen): la g.yas phyogs nas bskor nas/byang chub sems dpa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su byung ba'i dpal gyi spyi bor nub po//de nas byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor/de dag thams cad ngo mtshar tshar 'dzin cing /dga' 
77a1(phal chen): @#//pa thob par gyur te/'od zer gyi dra ba snang ba 'di lta bu byung bas na chos chen po brjod par ni 'gyur des so snyam nas/rtog pa'i blo dang thob par gyur to//'od zer de dag gis byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin 
77a2(phal chen): gshegs pa'i rigs su byung ba'i dpal gyi lus la thob par gyur ma thag tu//de nas de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su byung pa'i dpal pad mo'i stan las langs te//bla gos 
77a3(phal chen): phrag pa gcig la gzar nas//pad mo'i snying po de la pus mos g.yas pa'i lha nga btsugs nas/bcom ldan 'das gang na bder thal mos btud te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'du shes dang ldan pa'i yid la byed pas/de bzhin 
77a4(phal chen): gshegs pa la tshigs su bcad de gsol pa//rnam dag blo mnga' yon tan mkhyen pas 'phags pa//spyod yul chags pa med pa'i pha rol phyin brnyes//dus gsum mnyam pa'i chos nyid mnyam ston pa//khyod la bdag 
77a5(phal chen): ni sti stang rim gro bgyid do//mtshan nyid med pa'i spyod yul pha rol phyin brnyes//mtshan gyis shin tu bstan pa'i 'ong ston mdzad//dri med 'od chen 'od zer stong ldan 'gyed pa//bdud kyi sde rnams ma lus rab tu 'jom 
77a6(phal chen): mdzad//phyogs bcu kun na gang rnams ji snyed mchis pa'i//zhing rnams thams cad ma lus rab tu skyod kyang //thugs rje ldan pas su la 'ang 'jigs par mi mdzad//khyod kyi rdzu 'phrul mthu ni de 'dra de tshul//nam mkha'i 
77a7(phal chen): dbyings la mnyam pa nyid du rab bzhugs//de bzhin chos dbyings mnyam pa nyid kyang brnyes gyur//sems can stong phrag bye ba grol bar mdzad cing //sems can kun kyi sdig chen rab tu zhi mdzad//bskal pa stong du 
77a8(phal chen): brtul zhugs dam po spyod de//byang chub mchog gi lam ni yang dag bsgrubs nas//chags pa med pa'i ye shes spyod yul 'di brnyes//sangs rgyas kun dang rang bzhin gcig tu rab 'gyur//rnam par 'dren pa 
77b1(phal chen): khyod kyis 'di nas 'di bgye//'jig rten thams cad ma lus kun tu bskyod nas//cho 'phrul mtha' yas yang dag bstan par bgyis te//bdag gi lus 'dir de dag ma lus zhugs gyur//de la bdag gi blo gros 'di 
77b2(phal chen): ltar skyes te//chos rje khyod la bdag gis zhu ba'i dus bab//shin tu des pa'i chos la rab tu bslabs pa'i//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyang 'dus par gyur snyam//chos 'di dag pa 'dus pas rnam par dag pa//
77b3(phal chen): 'jig rten slob dpon mgon pos rab tu bsngags pa//ye shes mtha' yas ldan pa yul la chags med//de 'dri' mthu chen dpa' bo 'di dag 'dir phyin//de'i don 'jig rten slob dpon drang srong chen po//ye shes stobs 
77b4(phal chen): dang brtson 'grus mtha' yas byung gyur//byang chub sems dpa'i de dag snang bar mdzad gsol//chos rab bdag gis gang zhus de yang bshad gsol//drang srong chen po'i spyod yul 'di dag bstan cing //yang 
77b5(phal chen): dag ma nor brjod par nus pa gang lags//skyes bu khyu mchog rnams kyi sras ni gang lags//'jig rten mgon gyi gtso bo khyod kyis bshad gsol//tshigs su bcad pa de dag gsol ma thag tu//de'i tshe bcom 
77b6(phal chen): ldan 'das kyi zhal gyi sgo nas mi bsnyengs pa'i mthar phyin pa zhes bya ba'i 'od gzer//'khor kyi 'od gzer khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa dang ldan pa byung ste//des 'jig rten gyi khams 'byam klas pa thams cad ma

117b1(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams kyi tshad du ni stong gnyis pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams bris/stong gi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi tshadadu ni steng gi 'jig rten gyi khamasabris/gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams gyi tshad du ni gling bzhi pa'i 'jig

117b3(phal chen): kyi gling gi tshad du ni nub kyi ban lang spyod gi gling bris/byang gi sgra mi snyan gyi gling gi tshad du ni byang gi sgra mi snyan gyi gling bris/ri rab kyi tshad du ni ri rab bris/sa bla na spyod pa'i lha'i gzhal med khang gi tshad du ni sa bla na spyod pa'i lha'i 
117b4(phal chen): gzhal med khang rnams bris/'dod pa la spyod pa'i lha'i gzhal med/khang gi tshad du ni 'dod pa la spyod pa'i lha'igazhal med khang rnams bri/gzugs la spyod pa'i lha'i gzhal mad khang gi tshad du ni gzugs la spyod pa'i lha'igazhal

209a2(phal chen): gtan 'cha' yang 'dod pa'i dga' ba thams cad kyis rtse zhing longs spyod par sems can la rnam par ston pas gcig tu dga' bar bzhag cing mos pa/khams gsum thams cad las phyir ltas kyang sems can 
209a3(phal chen): thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba'i bsam pa ma btang pa/rtag tu chos kyi dga' bas dga' zhing mos kyang lhag pa'i bu mo brgya phrag stong gi sgra rab tu byung ba dang rtse ba mchan dang dpe byad bzang po dang /dge ba'i mchog 
209a4(phal chen): brgya phrag stong gis bskrun pa'i lus dang ldan yang dbul ba thams cad kyi tshul yongs su ston pa/rtag tu kha na ma tho ba med pa'i las dge ba spyad kyang sems can dmyal ba dang /byol song gi skye gnas dang /gshen rje'i 'jig 
209a5(phal chen): rten du skye ba yang yongs su ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i mchog thob kyang /byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes kyi lus las gyen du ma g.yos pa dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar byang chub sems 
209a6(phal chen): dpa' rnams ni ye shes mu med pa dang ldan pas/de'i phyir de dag nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis kyang khong du chud par dka' na sems can byis pa gzhan gyis lta smos kyang ci bgos te/'di ni byang chub

209a8(phal chen): nas sdig pa'i lta ba'i bar dang bral bas byams pa phun sum tshogs shing yang dag pa'i lta ba la zhugs pa yin te//kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar lus dang ngag dang yid kyi las ye shes sngon du 'gro ba dag ldan pa 'di ni byang
